        <body>
            <div n="1" type="sthāna">

                <div n="1" type="adhyāya">


                    <pb facs="87" n="1a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.1"><l> athāto vedotpattim adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||1|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ ||2|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.3"><l> atha khalu bhagavantam amaravaram
                        ṛṣigaṇaparivṛtam āśramasthaṃ kāśirājaṃ divodāsaṃ dhanvantarim
                            aupadhenava<pc> </pc>vaitaraṇaurabhra<pc> </pc>pauṣkalāvata<pc> </pc>karavīrya(ra)<pc> </pc>gopurarakṣita<pc> </pc>suśruta<pc> </pc>prabhṛtaya ūcuḥ
                        ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="88" n="2a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.4"><l> bhagavan śārīramānasāgantubhir vyādhibhir
                        vividhavedanābhighātopadrutān sanāthān apy anāthavad viceṣṭamānān
                        vikrośataś ca mānavān abhisamīkṣya manasi naḥ pīḍā bhavati, </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.4b"><l> teṣāṃ sukhaiṣiṇāṃ rogopaśamārtham ātmanaś ca
                        prāṇayātrārthaṃ prajāhitahetor āyurvedaṃ śrotum icchāma
                        ihopadiśyamānam, atrāyattam aihikam āmuṣmikaṃ ca śreyaḥ;
                        tadbhagavantam upapannāḥ smaḥ śiṣyatveneti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.5"><l> tān uvāca bhagavān- svāgataṃ vaḥ; sarva
                        evāmīmāṃsyā adhyāpyāś ca bhavanto vatsāḥ ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.6"><l> iha khalv āyurvedaṃ nāmopāṅgam
                        atharvavedasyānutpādyaiva prajāḥ ślokaśatasahasram adhyāyasahasraṃ
                        ca kṛtavān svayambhūḥ, tato 'lpāyuṣṭvam alpamedhastvaṃ cālokya
                        narāṇāṃ bhūyo 'ṣṭadhā praṇītavān ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.7"><l> tad yathā śalyaṃ, śālākyaṃ, kāyacikitsā,
                        bhūtavidyā, kaumārabhṛtyam, agadatantraṃ, rasāyanatantraṃ,
                        vājīkaraṇatantram iti ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="89" n="3a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.1"><l> athāsya pratyaṅgalakṣaṇasamāsaḥ |8| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.1a"><l> tatra, śalyaṃ nāma
                        vividhatṛṇakāṣṭhapāṣāṇapāṃśulohaloṣṭāsthibālanakhapūyāsrāvaduṣṭavraṇāntargarbhaśalyoddharaṇārthaṃ
                        , yantraśastrakṣārāgnipraṇidhānavraṇaviniścayārthaṃ ca (1) |8|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.2"><l> śālākyaṃ nāmordhvajatrugatānāṃ
                        śravaṇanayanavadanaghrāṇādisaṃśritānāṃ vyādhīnām upaśamanārtham
                        (2) |8| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.3"><l> kāyacikitsā nāma sarvāṅgasaṃśritānāṃ vyādhīnāṃ
                        jvararaktapittaśoṣonmādāpasmārakuṣṭhamehātisārādīnām
                        upaśamanārtham (3) |8| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.4"><l> bhūtavidyā nāma
                        devāsuragandharvayakṣarakṣaḥpitṛpiśācanāgagrahādyupasṛṣṭacetasāṃ
                        śāntikarmabaliharaṇādigrahopaśamanārtham (4) |8|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.5"><l> kaumārabhṛtyaṃ nāma
                        kumārabharaṇadhātrīkṣīradoṣasaṃśodhanārthaṃ
                        duṣṭastanyagrahasamutthānāṃ ca vyādhīnām upaśamanārtham (5)
                        |8|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.6"><l> agadatantraṃ nāma
                        sarpakīṭalūtāmūṣakādidaṣṭaviṣavyañjanārthaṃ
                        vividhaviṣasaṃyogopaśamanārthaṃ ca (6) |8|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.7"><l> rasāyanatantraṃ nāma
                        vayaḥsthāpanamāyurmedhābalakaraṃ rogāpaharaṇasamarthaṃ ca (7) |8| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.8"><l> vājīkaraṇatantraṃ
                        nāmālpaduṣṭakṣīṇaviśuṣkaretasām
                        āpyāyanaprasādopacayajanananimittaṃ praharṣajananārthaṃ ca (8)
                        ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.9"><l> evam ayam āyurvedo 'ṣṭāṅga upadiśyate; atra
                        kasmai kim ucyatām iti ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="90" n="4a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.10"><l> ta ūcuḥ- asmākaṃ sarveṣām eva śalyajñānaṃ mūlaṃ
                        kṛtvopadiśatu bhagavāniti ||10||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.11"><l> sa uvācaivam astv iti ||11||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.12"><l> ta ūcur bhūyo 'pi bhagavantam asmākam
                        ekamatīnāṃ matam abhisamīkṣya suśruto bhagavantaṃ prakṣyati, asmai
                        copadiśyamānaṃ vayam apy upadhārayiṣyāmaḥ ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.13"><l> sa uvācaivam astv iti ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.14-15"><l> vatsa suśruta! iha khalv āyurvedaprayojanaṃ
                        vyādhyupasṛṣṭānāṃ vyādhiparimokṣaḥ, svasthasya rakṣaṇaṃ ca ||14||
                        ‘āyur asmin vidyate, anena vā''yur vindanti’ ity āyurvedaḥ
                        ||15||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.16"><l> tasyāṅgavaram ādyaṃ pratyakṣāgamānumānopamānair
                        aviruddham ucyamānam upadhāraya ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="90" n="4b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.17"><l> etad dhy aṅgaṃ prathamaṃ,
                        prāgabhighātavraṇasaṃrohād yajñaśiraḥsandhānāc ca | śrūyate hi
                        yathā --- “rudreṇa yajñasya śiraś chinnam iti, tato devā
                        aśvināvabhigamyocuḥ- bhagavantau naḥ śreṣṭhatamau yuvāṃ
                        bhaviṣyathaḥ, bhavadbhyāṃ yajñasya śiraḥ sandhātavyam iti | tāv
                        ūcatur evam astv iti | atha tayor arthe devā indraṃ yajñabhāgena
                        prāsādayan | tābhyāṃ yajñasya śiraḥ saṃhitam” iti ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.18"><l> aṣṭāsvapi
                        cāyurvedatantreṣvetadevādhikamabhimatam,
                        āśukriyākaraṇādyantraśastrakṣārāgnipraṇidhānāt sarvatantrasāmānyāc
                        ca ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="91" n="5a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.19"><l> tad idaṃ śāśvataṃ puṇyaṃ svargyaṃ yaśasyam
                        āyuṣyaṃ vṛttikaraṃ ceti ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.20"><l> brahmā provāca tataḥ prajāpatir adhijage,
                        tasmād aśvinau, aśvibhyām indraḥ, indrād ahaṃ, mayā tv iha
                        pradeyam arthibhyaḥ prajāhitahetoḥ ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.21"><l> bhavati cātra ahaṃ hi dhanvantarir ādidevo
                        jarārujāmṛtyuharo 'marāṇām |
                        <caesura/>
                         śalyāṅgam aṅgair aparair upetaṃ prāpto 'smi gāṃ bhūya ihopadeṣṭum
                        ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.22"><l> asmiñchāstre pañcamahābhūtaśarīrisamavāyaḥ
                        puruṣa ity ucyate | tasmin kriyā, so 'dhiṣṭhānaṃ; kasmāt? lokasya
                        dvaividhyāta ; loko hi dvividhaḥ sthāvaro jaṅgamaśca;
                        dvividhātmaka evāgneyaḥ, saumyaśca, tadbhūyastvāt ; pañcātmako vā;
                        tatra caturvidho bhūtagrāmaḥ saṃsvedajajarāyujāṇḍajodbhijjasañjñaḥ
                        ; tatra puruṣaḥ pradhānaṃ, tasyopakaraṇamanyat; tasmāt puruṣo
                        'dhiṣṭhānam ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="92" n="6a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.23"><l> tad duḥkhasaṃyogā vyādhaya ucyante ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.24"><l> te caturvidhāḥ- āgantavaḥ, śārīrāḥ, mānasāḥ,
                        svābhāvikāśceti ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.1"><l> teṣāṃ āgantavo 'bhighātanimittāḥ</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.2"><l> śārīrās tv annapānamūlā
                        vātapittakaphaśoṇitasannipātavaiṣamyanimittāḥ</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.3"><l> mānasās tu
                        krodhaśokabhayaharṣaviṣāderṣyābhyasūyādainyamātsaryakāmalobhaprabhṛtaya
                        icchādveṣabhedair bhavanti</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.25.4"><l> svābhāvikās tu
                        kṣutpipāsājarāmṛtyunidrāprakṛ(bhṛ)tayaḥ ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="93" n="7a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.26"><l> ta ete manaḥśarīrādhiṣṭhānāḥ ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.27"><l> teṣāṃ saṃśodhanasaṃśamanāhārācārāḥ samyak
                        prayuktā nigrahahetavaḥ ||27|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.28"><l> prāṇināṃ punar mūlam āhāro balavarṇaujasāṃ ca |
                        sa ṣaṭsu raseṣv āyattaḥ; rasāḥ punar dravyāśrayāḥ; dravyāṇi punar
                        oṣadhayaḥ | tās tu dvividhāḥ- sthāvarā jaṅgamāś ca ||28|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.29"><l> tāsāṃ sthāvarāś caturvidhāḥ- vanaspatayo,
                        vṛkṣā, vīrudha, oṣadhaya iti | tāsu, apuṣpāḥ phalavanto
                        vanaspatayaḥ, puṣpaphalavanto vṛkṣāḥ, pratānavatyaḥ stambinyaś ca
                        vīrudhaḥ, phalapākaniṣṭhā oṣadhaya iti ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="94" n="8a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.30"><l> jaṅgamā khalv api caturvidhāḥ
                        jarāyujāṇḍajasvedajodbhijjāḥ | tatra paśumanuṣyavyālādayo
                        jarāyujāḥ, khagasarpasarīsṛpaprabhṛtayo 'ṇḍajāḥ,
                        kṛmikīṭapipīlikāprabhṛtayaḥ svedajāḥ, indragopamaṇḍūkaprabhṛtaya
                        udbhijjāḥ ||30|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.31"><l> tatra sthāvarebhyas
                        tvakpatrapuṣpaphalamūlakandaniryāsasvarasādayaḥ prayojanavantaḥ;
                        jaṅgamebhyaś carmanakharomarudhirādayaḥ ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.32"><l> pārthivāḥ
                        suvarṇarajatamaṇimuktāmanaḥśilāmṛtkapālādayaḥ ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.33"><l> kālakṛtāḥ
                        pravātanivātātapacchāyājyotsnātamaḥśītoṣṇavarṣāhorātrapakṣamāsartvayanādayaḥ
                        saṃvatsaraviśeṣāḥ ||33|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.34"><l> ta ete svabhāvata eva doṣāṇāṃ
                        sañcayaprakopapraśamapratīkārahetavaḥ prayojanavantaś ca ||34|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.35"><l> bhavanti cātra ślokāḥ-
                        <caesura/>
                         śārīrāṇāṃ vikārāṇām eṣa vargaś caturvidhaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakope praśame caiva hetur uktaścikitsakaiḥ ||35|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="95" n="9a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.36"><l> āgantavas tu ye rogās te dvidhā nipatanti hi |
                        <caesura/>
                         manasy anye śarīre 'nye teṣāṃ tu dvividhā kriyā ||36|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.37"><l> śarīrapatitānāṃ tu śārīravadupakramaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mānasānāṃ tu śabdādiriṣṭo vargaḥ sukhāvahaḥ ||37||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.38"><l> evam etat puruṣo vyādhir auṣadhaṃ kriyā kāla
                        iti catuṣṭayaṃ samāsena vyākhyātam | tatra puruṣagrahaṇāt
                        tatsambhavadravyasamūho bhūtādir uktas tadaṅgapratyaṅgavikalpāś ca
                        tvaṅmāṃsāsthisirāsnāyuprabhṛtayaḥ, vyādhigrahaṇād
                        vātapittakaphaśoṇitasannipātavaiṣamyanimittāḥ sarva eva vyādhayo
                        vyākhyātāḥ, auṣadhagrahaṇāddravyarasaguṇavīryavipākānāmādeśaḥ,
                        kriyāgrahaṇāt snehādīni cchedyādīni ca karmāṇi vyākhyātāni,
                        kālagrahaṇāt sarvakriyākālānāmādeśaḥ ||38|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.39"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         bījaṃ cikitsitasyaitatsamāsena prakīrtitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         saviṃśamadhyāyaśatamasya vyākhyā bhaviṣyati ||39|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="96" n="10a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.40"><l> tac ca saviṃśam adhyāyaśataṃ pañcasu sthāneṣu |
                        tatra sūtranidānaśārīracikitsitakalpeṣv arthavaśāt
                        saṃvibhajyottare tantre śeṣān arthān vakṣyāmaḥ ||40|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.41"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         svayambhuvā proktam idaṃ sanātanaṃ
                        <caesura/>
                         paṭhed dhi yaḥ kāśipatiprakāśitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa puṇyakarmā bhuvi pūjito nṛpair
                        <caesura/>
                         asukṣaye śakrasalokatāṃ vrajet ||41|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        vedotpattir nāma prathamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||1|| </l></lg>
                </div>


                <div n="2" type="adhyāya">

                    <head>[dvitīyo 'dhyāyaḥ|]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.1"><l> athātaḥ śiṣyopanayanīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.3"><l> brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyānām
                        anyatamamanvayavayaḥśīlaśauryaśaucācāravinayaśaktibalamedhādhṛtismṛtimatipratipattiyuktaṃ
                        tanujihvauṣṭhadantāgramṛjuvaktrākṣināsaṃ prasannacittavākceṣṭaṃ
                        kleśasahaṃ ca bhiṣak śiṣyam upanayet | ato viparītaguṇaṃ nopanayet
                        ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="97" n="11a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.4"><l> upanayanīyaṃ tu brāhmaṇaṃ praśasteṣu
                        tithikaraṇamuhūrtanakṣatreṣu praśastāyāṃ diśi śucau same deśe
                        caturhastaṃ caturasraṃ sthaṇḍilam upalipya gomayena, darbhaiḥ
                        saṃstīrya ratnapuṣpalājabhaktair devatāḥ pūjayitvā viprān bhiṣajaś
                        ca, tatrollikhyābhyukṣya ca dakṣiṇato brahmāṇaṃ sthāpayitvā'gnim
                        upasamādhāya, khadirapalāśadevadārubilvānāṃ samidbhiścaturṇāṃ vā
                        kṣīrivṛkṣāṇāṃ (nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthamadhūkānāṃ)
                        dadhimadhughṛtāktābhir dārvīhaumikena vidhinā sapraṇavābhir
                        mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ sruveṇājyāhutīr juhuyāt, tataḥ pratidaivatamṛṣīṃś
                        ca svāhākāraṃ kuryāt , śiṣyam api kārayet ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.5"><l> brāhmaṇas trayāṇāṃ varṇānām upanayanaṃ
                        kartumarhati, (rājanyo dvayasya, vaiśyo vaiśyasyaiveti;) śūdramapi
                        kulaguṇasampannaṃ mantravarjamanupanītamadhyāpayed ity eke ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.6"><l> tato 'gniṃ triḥ pariṇīyāgnisākṣikaṃ śiṣyaṃ
                        brūyāt-
                        kāmakrodhalobhamohamānāhaṅkārerṣyāpāruṣyapaiśunyānṛtālasyāyaśasyāni
                        hitvā, nīcanakharomṇā śucinā kaṣāyavāsasā
                        satyavratabrahmacaryābhivādanatatpareṇāvaśyaṃ bhavitavyaṃ,
                        madanumatasthānagamanaśayanāsanabhojanādhyayanapareṇa bhūtvā
                        matpriyahiteṣu vartitavyam; ato 'nyathā te vartamānasyādharmo
                        bhavati, aphalā ca vidyā, na ca prākāśyaṃ prāpnoti ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.7"><l> ahaṃ vā tvayi samyagvartamāne yady anyathādarśī
                        syām enobhāg bhaveyam aphalavidyaś ca ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.8"><l>
                        dvijagurudaridramitrapravrajitopanatasādhvanāthābhyupagatānāṃ
                        cātmabāndhavānām iva svabhaiṣajaiḥ pratikartavyam, evaṃ sādhu
                        bhavati; vyādhaśākunika<pb facs="98" n="12a"/>patitapāpakāriṇāṃ ca
                        na pratikartavyam; evaṃ vidyā prakāśate mitra<pc> </pc>yaśo<pc> </pc>dharmārtha<pc> </pc>kāmāṃś ca prāpnoti
                        ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.9"><l> bhavataś cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛṣṇe 'ṣṭamī tannidhane 'hanī dve
                        <caesura/>
                         śukle tathā'pyevamahardvisandhyam |
                        <caesura/>
                         akālavidyutstanayitnughoṣe
                        <caesura/>
                         svatantrarāṣṭrakṣitipavyathāsu ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.10"><l> śmaśānayānādyatanāhaveṣu
                        <caesura/>
                         mahotsavautpātikadarśaneṣu |
                        <caesura/>
                         nādhyeyamanyeṣu ca yeṣu viprā
                        <caesura/>
                         nādhīyate nāśucinā ca nityam ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.2.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        śiṣyopanayanīyo nāma dvitīyo 'dhyāyaḥ ||2|| </l></lg>

                <div n="3" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.1"><l> athāto 'dhyayanasaṃpradānīyam adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.3"><l> prāgabhihitaṃ saviṃśam adhyāyaśataṃ pañcasu
                        sthāneṣu | tatra sūtrasthānamadhyāyāḥ ṣaṭcatvāriṃśat, ṣoḍaśa
                        nidānāni, daśa śārīrāṇi, catvāriṃśaccikitsitāni, aṣṭau kalpāḥ,
                        taduttaraṃ ṣaṭṣaṣṭiḥ ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.4"><l> vedotpattiḥ śiṣyanayas tathā'dhyayanadānikaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prabhāṣaṇāgraharaṇāvṛtucaryā'tha yāntrikaḥ ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.5"><l> śastrāvacāraṇaṃ yogyā viśikhā kṣārakalpanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         agnikarma jalaukākhyo hy adhyāyo raktavarṇanam ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.6"><l> doṣadhātumalādyānāṃ vijñānādhyāya eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         karṇavyadhāmapakvaiṣāvālepo vraṇyupāsanam ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.7"><l> hitāhito vraṇapraśno vraṇāsrāvaś ca yaḥ pṛthak |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛtyākṛtyavidhirvyādhisamuddeśīya eva ca ||7||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.8"><l> viniścayaḥ śastravidhau pranaṣṭajñānikas tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         śalyoddhṛtirvraṇajñānaṃ dūtasvapnanidarśanam ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="99" n="13a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.9"><l> pañcendriyaṃ tathā chāyā svabhāvādvaikṛtaṃ tathā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         avāraṇo yuktasenīya āturakramabhūmikau ||9||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.10"><l> miśrakākhyo dravyagaṇaḥ saṃśuddhau śamane ca
                        yaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dravyādīnāṃ ca vijñānaṃ viśeṣo dravyago 'paraḥ ||10||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.11"><l> rasajñānaṃ vamanārthamadhyāyo recanāya ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         dravadravyavidhistadvadannapānavidhis tathā ||11||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.12"><l> sūcanāt sūtraṇāccaiva savanāccārthasantateḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ṣaṭcatvāriṃśadadhyāyaṃ sūtrasthānaṃ pracakṣate ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="99" n="12b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.13"><l> vātavyādhikamarśāṃsi sāśmariś ca bhagandaraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kuṣṭhamehodaraṃ mūḍho vidradhiḥ parisarpaṇam ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.14"><l> granthivṛddhikṣudraśūkabhagnāś ca mukharogikam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         hetulakṣaṇanirdeśānnidānānīti ṣoḍaśa ||14||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.15"><l> bhūtacintā rajaḥśuddhirgarbhāvakrāntireva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         vyākaraṇaṃ ca garbhasya śarīrasya ca yatsmṛtam ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.16"><l> pratyekaṃ marmanirdeśaḥ sirāvarṇanam eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         sirāvyadho dhamanīnāṃ garbhiṇyā vyākṛtis tathā ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.17"><l> nirdiṣṭāni daśaitāni śārīrāṇi maharṣiṇā |
                        <caesura/>
                         vijñānārthaṃ śarīrasya bhiṣajāṃ yoginām api ||17||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.18"><l> dvivraṇīyo vraṇaḥ sadyo bhagnānāṃ vātarogikam |
                        <caesura/>
                         mahāvātikamarśāṃsi sāśmariś ca bhagandaraḥ ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.19"><l> kuṣṭhānāṃ mahatāṃ cāpi maihikaṃ paiḍikaṃ tathā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         madhumehacikitsā ca tathā codariṇām api ||19||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.20"><l> mūḍhagarbhacikitsā ca vidradhīnāṃ visarpiṇām |
                        <caesura/>
                         granthivṛddhyupadaṃśānāṃ tathā ca kṣudrarogiṇām ||20||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.21"><l> śūkadoṣacikitsā ca tathā ca mukharogiṇām |
                        <caesura/>

                    <pb facs="100" n="13a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.22"><l> vājīkaraṃ ca yat kṣīṇe sarvābādhaśamo 'pi ca |

                         medhāyuṣkaraṇaṃ cāpi svabhāvavyādhivāraṇam ||22||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.23"><l> nivṛttasantāpakaraṃ kīrtitaṃ ca rasāyanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         snehopayaugikaḥ svedo vamane savirecane ||23||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.24"><l> tayor vyāpaccikitsā ca netrabastivibhāgikaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         netrabastivipatsiddhis tathā cottarabastikaḥ ||24||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.25"><l> nirūhakramasañjñaś ca tathaivāturasañjñakaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhūmanasyavidhiś cāntyaścatvāriṃśaditi smṛtāḥ ||25||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.26"><l> prāyaścittaṃ praśamanaṃ cikitsā śāntikarma ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         paryāyās tasya nirdeśāccikitsāsthānamucyate ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.27"><l> annasya rakṣā vijñānaṃ sthāvarasyetarasya ca |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.28"><l> dundubhermūṣikāṇāṃ ca kīṭānāṃ kalpa eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         aṣṭau kalpāḥ samākhyātā viṣabheṣajakalpanāt ||28||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.29"><l> adhyāyānāṃ śataṃ viṃśamevam etad udīritam
                        <caesura/>
                         ataḥ paraṃ svanāmnaiva tantram uttaram ucyate ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.30"><l> adhikṛtya kṛtaṃ yasmāttantrametadupadravān |
                        <caesura/>
                         aupadravika ity eṣa tasyāgryatvānnirucyate ||30||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.31"><l> sandhau vartmani śukle ca kṛṣṇe sarvatra
                        dṛṣṭiṣu |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃvijñānārthamadhyāyā gadānāṃ tu prati prati ||31||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.32"><l> cikitsāpravibhāgīyo vātābhiṣyandavāraṇaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         paittasya ślaiṣmikasyāpi raudhirasya tathaiva ca ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.33"><l> lekhyabhedyaniṣedhau ca chedyānāṃ vartmadṛṣṭiṣu
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kriyākalpo 'bhighātaś ca karṇotthāstaccikitsitam ||33||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.34"><l> ghrāṇotthānāṃ ca vijñānaṃ tadgadapratiṣedhanam
                        |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.35"><l> cikitsā tadgadānāṃ ca śālākyaṃ tantram ucyate
                        |35|
                        <caesura/>

                        niṣedhanam ||35||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.36"><l> apasmāraśakunyoś ca revatyāś ca punaḥ pṛthak |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.37"><l> naigameṣacikitsā ca grahotpattiḥ sayonijā |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.38"><l> jvarātisāraśoṣāṇāṃ gulmahṛdrogiṇām api |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāṇḍūnāṃ raktapittasya mūrcchāyāḥ pānajāś ca ye ||38|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.39"><l> tṛṣṇāyāś chardihikkānāṃ niṣedhaḥ śvāsakāsayoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         svarabhedacikitsā ca kṛmyudāvartinoḥ pṛthak ||39|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.40"><l> visūcikārocakayor mūtrāghātavikṛcchrayoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         iti kāyacikitsāyāḥ śeṣam atra prakīrtitam ||40||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.41"><l> amānuṣaniṣedhaś ca tathā''pasmāriko 'paraḥ |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.42"><l> rasabhedāḥ svasthavṛttaṃ yuktayastāntrikāśca
                        yāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         doṣabhedā iti jñeyā adhyāyāstantrabhūṣaṇāḥ ||42|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.43"><l> śreṣṭhatvād uttaraṃ hy etat tantram āhur
                        maharṣayaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bahvarthasaṅgrahāc chreṣṭham uttaraṃ vā'pi paścimam ||43|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.44"><l> śālākyatantraṃ kaumāraṃ cikitsā kāyikī ca yā |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.45"><l> vājīkaraṃ cikitsāsu rasāyanavidhis tathā |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.46"><l> ity aṣṭāṅgam idaṃ tantramādidevaprakāśitam |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.47"><l> etad dhy avaśyam adhyeyam, adhītya ca karmāpy
                        avaśyam upāsitavyam, ubhayajño hi bhiṣag rājārho bhavati ||47|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="102" n="16a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.48"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>
                         sa muhyaty āturaṃ prāpya prāpya bhīrur ivāhavam ||48|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.49"><l> yas tu karmasu niṣṇāto dhārṣṭyāc
                        chāstrabahiṣkṛtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa satsu pūjāṃ nāpnoti vadhaṃ carcchati rājataḥ ||49||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.50"><l> ubhāv etāv anipuṇāv asamarthau svakarmaṇi |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.51"><l> oṣadhyo 'mṛtakalpās tu śastrāśaniviṣopamāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhavanty ajñair upahṛtās tasmād etān vivarjayet ||51|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.52"><l> snehādiṣvanabhijño yaśchedyādiṣu ca karmasu |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa nihanti janaṃ lobhāt kuvaidyo nṛpadoṣataḥ ||52|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.53"><l> yas tūbhayajño matimān sa samartho 'rthasādhane
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         āhave karma nirvoḍhuṃ dvicakraḥ syandano yathā ||53|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.54"><l> atha vatsa! tad etad adhyeyaṃ yathā
                        tathopadhāraya mayā procyamānaṃ atha śucaye
                        kṛtottarāsaṅgāyāvyākulāyopasthitāyādhyayanakāle śiṣyāya yathāśakti
                        gururupadiśet padaṃ pādaṃ ślokaṃ vā; te ca padapādaślokā bhūyaḥ
                        krameṇānusandheyāḥ, evam ekaikaśo ghaṭayed ātmanā cānupaṭhet;
                        adrutam avilambitam aviśaṅkitam ananunāsikaṃ suvyaktākṣaram
                        apīḍitavarṇam akṣibhruvauṣṭhahastair anabhinītaṃ susaṃskṛtaṃ
                        nātyuccair nātinīcaiś ca svaraiḥ paṭhet | na cāntareṇa kaścid
                        vrajet tayor adhīyānayoḥ ||54|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="103" n="17a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.55"><l> bhavataś cātra |
                        <caesura/>
                         śucirguruparo dakṣastandrānidrāvivarjitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         paṭhann etena vidhinā śiṣyaḥ śāstrāntam āpnuyāt ||55|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.3.56"><l> vāksauṣṭhave 'rthavijñāne prāgalbhye
                        karmanaipuṇe |
                        <caesura/>

                <div n="4" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.1"><l> athātaḥ prabhāṣaṇīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.2"><l> yāthovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.3"><l> adhigatam apy adhyayanam aprabhāṣitam arthataḥ
                        kharasya candanabhāra iva kevalaṃ pariśramakaraṃ bhavati ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.4"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         yathā kharaś candanabhāravāhī
                        <caesura/>
                         bhārasya vettā na tu candanasya |
                        <caesura/>
                         evaṃ hi śāstrāṇi bahūny adhītya
                        <caesura/>
                         cārtheṣu mūḍhāḥ kharavad vahanti ||4||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.5"><l> tasmāt saviṃśama dhyāyaśatam anupadapādaślokam
                        anuvarṇayitavyam anuśrotavyaṃ ca; kasmāt? sūkṣmā hi
                        dravyarasaguṇavīryavipākadoṣadhātumalāśayamarmasirāsnāyusandhyasthigarbhasambhavadravyasamūhavibhāgāstathā
                        pranaṣṭaśalyoddharaṇavraṇaviniścayabhagnavikalpāḥ
                        sādhyayāpyapratyākhyeyatā ca vikārāṇāmevamādayaś cānye viśeṣāḥ
                        sahasraśo ye vicintyamānā vimalavipulabuddherapi
                        buddhimākulīkuryuḥ kiṃ punaralpabuddheḥ, tasmād
                        avaśyamanupadapādaślokamanuvarṇayitavyamanuśrotavyaṃ ca ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="104" n="18a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.6"><l> anyaśāstropapannānāṃ
                        cārthānāmihopanītānāmarthavaśātteṣāṃ tadvidyebhya eva
                        vyākhyānamanuśrotavyaṃ, kasmāt? na hyekasmin śāstre śakyaḥ
                        sarvaśāstrāṇāmavarodhaḥ kartum ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.7"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         ekaṃ śāstramadhīyāno na vidyācchāstraniścayam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmād bahuśrutaḥ śāstraṃ vijānīyāccikitsakaḥ ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.8"><l> śāstraṃ gurumukhodgīrṇamādāyopāsya cāsakṛt |
                        <caesura/>
                         yaḥ karma kurute vaidyaḥ sa vaidyo 'nye tu taskarāḥ ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.9"><l> aupadhenavam aurabhraṃ sauśrutaṃ pauṣkalāvatam |
                        <caesura/>
                         śeṣāṇāṃ śalyatantrāṇāṃ mūlāny etāni nirdiśet ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.4.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        prabhāṣaṇīyo nāma caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <head>śaṣṭho 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>

                    <pb facs="109" n="21b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.1"><l> athāta ṛtucaryam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.3"><l> kālo hi nāma (bhagavān) svayambhur
                        anādimadhyanidhanaḥ | atra rasavyāpatsampattī jīvitamaraṇe ca
                        manuṣyāṇām āyatte | sa sūkṣmām api kalāṃ na līyata iti kālaḥ,
                        saṅkalayati kālayati vā bhūtānīti kālaḥ ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="110" n="24a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.4"><l> tasya saṃvatsarātmano bhagavān ādityo
                        gativiśeṣeṇākṣinimeṣakāṣṭhākalāmuhūrtāhorātrapakṣamāsartvayana<pc> </pc>saṃvatsara<pc> </pc>yuga<pc> </pc>pravibhāgaṃ
                        karoti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.5"><l> tatra laghvakṣaroccāraṇamātro 'kṣinimeṣaḥ,
                        pañcadaśā'kṣinimeṣāḥ kāṣṭhā, triṃśatkāṣṭhāḥ kalā, viṃśatikalo
                        muhūrtaḥ kalādaśabhāgaś ca, triṃśanmuhūrtamahorātraṃ,
                        pañcadaśāhorātrāṇi pakṣaḥ, sa ca dvividhaḥ- śuklaḥ kṛṣṇaś ca, tau
                        māsaḥ ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.6"><l> tatra māghādayo dvādaśa māsāḥ, dvimāsikam ṛtuṃ
                        kṛtvā ṣaḍṛtavo bhavanti; te śiśiravasantagrīṣmavarṣāśaraddhemantāḥ
                        | teṣāṃ tapastapasyau śiśiraḥ, madhumādhavau vasantaḥ, śuciśukrau
                        grīṣmaḥ, nabhonabhasyau varṣāḥ, iṣorjau śarat, sahaḥsahasyau
                        hemanta iti ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.7"><l> ta ete śītoṣṇavarṣalakṣaṇāś candrādityayoḥ
                        kālavibhāgakaratvād ayane dve bhavato dakṣiṇam uttaraṃ ca | tayor
                        dakṣiṇaṃ varṣāśaraddhemantāḥ; teṣu bhagavā<pb facs="111" n="15a"/>n āpyāyate somaḥ, amlalavaṇamadhurāś ca rasā balavanto bhavanti,
                        uttarottaraṃ ca sarvaprāṇināṃ balam abhivardhate | uttaraṃ ca
                        śiśiravasantagrīṣmāḥ, teṣu bhagavān āpyāyate 'rkaḥ,
                        tiktakaṣāyakaṭukāś ca rasā balavanto bhavanti, uttarottaraṃ ca
                        sarvaprāṇināṃ balam upahīyate ||7||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.8"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         śītāṃśuḥ kledayatyurvīṃ vivasvān śoṣayatyapi |
                        <caesura/>
                         tāv ubhāv api saṃśritya vāyuḥ pālayati prajāḥ ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.9"><l> atha khalv ayane dve yugapat saṃvatsaro bhavati
                        | te tu pañca yugam iti sañjñāṃ labhante | sa eṣa nimeṣādir
                        yugaparyantaḥ kālaś cakravat parivartamānaḥ kālacakram ity ucyata
                        ity eke ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="111" n="25b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.10"><l> iha tu varṣāśaraddhemantavasantagrīṣmaprāvṛṣaḥ
                        ṣaḍṛtavo bhavanti, doṣopacayaprakopopaśamanimittaṃ; te tu
                        bhādrapadādyena dvimāsikena vyākhyātāḥ; tad yathā-
                        bhādrapadāśvayujau varṣāḥ, kārtikamārgaśīrṣau śarat, pauṣamāghau
                        hemantaḥ, phālgunacaitrau vasantaḥ, vaiśākhajyeṣṭhau grīṣmaḥ;
                        āṣāḍhaśrāvaṇau prāvṛḍ iti ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.11"><l> tatra, varṣāsv oṣadhayas taruṇyo 'lpavīryā āpaś
                        cāpraśāntāḥ kṣitimalaprāyāḥ, tā upayujyamānā nabhasi meghāvatate
                        jalapraklinnāyāṃ bhūmau klinnadehānāṃ prāṇināṃ
                        śīta<pc> </pc>vāta<pc> </pc>viṣṭambhitāgnīnāṃ vidahyante, <pb facs="112" n="26a"/>vidāhāt
                            pittasañcayam āpādayanti; sa sañcayaḥ śaradi
                            praviralameghe viyaty upaśuṣyati paṅke 'rka<pc> </pc>kiraṇa<pc> </pc>pravilāyitaḥ
                        paittikān vyādhīñ janayati | tā evauṣadhayaḥ kālapariṇāmāt
                        pariṇatavīryā balavatyo hemante bhavanty āpaś ca praśāntāḥ snigdhā
                        atyarthaṃ gurvyāś ca, tā upayujyamānā mandakiraṇatvād bhānoḥ
                        satuṣārapavanopastambhitadehānāṃ dehinām avidagdhāḥ snehāc chaityād
                        gauravād upalepāc ca śleṣmasañcayam āpādayanti; sa sañcayo vasante
                        'rkaraśmipravilāyita īṣatstabdhadehānāṃ dehināṃ ślaiṣmikān
                        vyādhīñ janayati | tā evauṣadhayo nidāghe niḥsārā rūkṣā atimātraṃ
                        laghvyo bhavanty āpaś ca, tā upayujyamānāḥ
                        sūryapratāpopaśoṣitadehānāṃ dehināṃ raukṣyāl laghutvāc ca vāyoḥ
                        sañcayam āpādayanti; sa sañcayaḥ prāvṛṣi cātyarthaṃ jalopaklinnāyāṃ
                        bhūmau klinnadehānāṃ dehināṃ śītavātavarṣerito vātikān
                        vyādhīñ janayati | evam eṣa doṣāṇāṃ sañcayaprakopahetur uktaḥ
                        ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="113" n="27a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.12"><l> tatra varṣāhemantagrīṣmeṣu sañcitānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ
                        śaradvasantaprāvṛṭsu ca prakupitānāṃ nirharaṇaṃ kartavyam ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.13"><l> tatra paittikānāṃ vyādhīnām upaśamo hemante,
                        ślaiṣmikāṇāṃ nidāghe, vātikānāṃ śaradi , svabhāvata eva; ta ete
                        sañcayaprakopopaśamā vyākhyātāḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.14"><l> tatra, pūrvāhṇe vasantasya liṅgaṃ, madhyāhne
                        grīṣmasya, aparāhṇe prāvṛṣaḥ , pradoṣe vārṣikaṃ, śāradam
                        ardharātre, pratyuṣasi haimantam upalakṣayet; evam ahorātram api
                        varṣam iva śītoṣṇavarṣalakṣaṇaṃ doṣopacayaprakopopaśamair jānīyāt
                        ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.15"><l> tatra, avyāpanneṣu ṛtuṣv avyāpannā oṣadhayo
                        bhavanty āpaś ca; tā upayujyamānāḥ prāṇāyurbalavīryaujaskaryo
                        bhavanti ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.16"><l> teṣāṃ punar vyāpado 'dṛṣṭa<pb facs="114"
                                n="26a"/>kāritāḥ, śītoṣṇavātavarṣāṇi khalu viparītāny oṣadhīr vyāpādayanty apaś ca ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.17"><l> tāsām upayogād vividharogaprādurbhāvo marako vā
                        bhaved iti ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.18"><l> tatra, avyāpannānām oṣadhīnām apāṃ copayogaḥ
                        ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.19"><l> kadācid avyāpanneṣv api ṛtuṣu
                        kṛtyābhiśāparakṣaḥkrodhādharmair upadhvasyante janapadāḥ,
                        viṣauṣadhipuṣpagandhena vā vāyunopanītenākramyate yo deśas tatra
                        doṣaprakṛtyaviśeṣeṇa kāsaśvāsavamathupratiśyāyaśirorugjvarair
                        upatapyante, grahanakṣatracaritair vā,
                        gṛhadāraśayanāsanayānavāhanamaṇiratnopakaraṇagarhitalakṣaṇanimittaprādurbhāvair
                        vā ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.20"><l> tatra,
                            sthāna<pc> </pc>parityāga<pc> </pc>śānti<pc> </pc>karma<pc>
                            </pc>prāyaścitta<pc> </pc>maṅgala<pc> </pc>japa<pc>
                            </pc>homopahārejyāñjali<pc> </pc>namaskāra<pc> </pc>tapo<pc>
                            </pc>niyama<pc> </pc>dayā<pc> </pc>dāna<pc> </pc>dīkṣābhyupagama<pc> </pc>devatā<pc> </pc>brāhmaṇa<pc> </pc>guru<pc> </pc>parair bhavitavyam, evaṃ sādhu bhavati ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.21"><l> ata ūrdhvam avyāpannānāmṛtūnāṃ lakṣaṇāny
                        upadekṣyāmaḥ ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.22"><l> vāyur vāty uttaraḥ śīto rajodhūmākulā diśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         channastuṣāraiḥ savitā himānaddhā jalāśayāḥ ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.23"><l> darpitā dhvāṅkṣakhaṅgāhvamahiṣorabhrakuñjarāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         rodhrapriyaṅgupunnāgāḥ puṣpitā himasāhvaye ||23||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="115" n="21a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.24"><l> śiśire śītamadhikaṃ vātavṛṣṭyākulā diśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śeṣaṃ hemantavat sarvaṃ vijñeyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ budhaiḥ ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.25"><l> siddhavidyādharavadhūcaraṇālaktakāṅkite |
                        <caesura/>
                         malaye candanalatāpariṣvaṅgādhivāsite ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.26"><l> vāti kāmijanānandajanano 'naṅgadīpanaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dampatyor mānabhiduro vasante dakṣiṇo 'nilaḥ ||26||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.27"><l> diśo vasante vimalāḥ kānanair upaśobhitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kiṃśukāmbhojabakulacūtāśokādipuṣpitaiḥ ||27||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.28"><l> kokilāṣaṭpadagaṇair upagītā manoharāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dakṣiṇānilasaṃvītāḥ sumukhāḥ pallavojjvalāḥ ||28||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.29"><l> grīṣme tīkṣṇāṃśurādityo māruto naiṟuto 'sukhaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhūs taptā saritas tanvyo diśaḥ prajvalitā iva ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.30"><l> bhrāntacakrāhvayugalāḥ payaḥpānākulā mṛgāḥ |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.31"><l> prāvṛṣy ambaram ānaddhaṃ paścimānilakarṣitaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ambudair vidyududdyotaprasrutais tumulasvanaiḥ ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="115" n="29b"/>

                        <caesura/>
                         kadambanīpakuṭajasarjaketakabhūṣitā ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.33"><l> tatra varṣāsu nadyo 'mbhaśchannokhātataṭadrumāḥ

                        <caesura/>
                         vāpy aḥ protphullakumudanīlotpalavirājitāḥ ||33|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.35"><l> babhrur uṣṇaḥ śarady arkaḥ śvetābhravimalaṃ
                        nabhaḥ |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.6.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne ṛtucaryā

                <div n="7" type="adhyāya">
                    <head>saptamo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.1"><l> athāto yantravidhim adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.3"><l> yantraśatam ekottaram; atra hastam eva
                        pradhānatamaṃ yantrāṇām avagaccha, (kiṃ kāraṇaṃ ? yasmādd hastād
                        ṛte yantrāṇām apravṛttir eva) tadadhīnatvād yantrakarmaṇām ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.4"><l> tatra, manaḥśarīrābādhakarāṇi śalyāni; teṣāṃ
                        āharaṇopāyo yantrāṇi ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.5"><l> tāni ṣaṭprakārāṇi; tad yathā- svastikayantrāṇi,
                        sandaṃśayantrāṇi, tālayantrāṇi, nāḍīyantrāṇi, śalākāyantrāṇi,
                        upayantrāṇi ceti ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.6"><l> tatra caturviṃśatiḥ svastikayantrāṇi , dve
                        sandaṃśayantre, dve eva tālayantre, viṃśatirnāḍyaḥ, aṣṭāviṃśatiḥ
                        śalākāḥ, pañcaviṃśatirupayantrāṇi ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="117" n="31a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.7"><l> tāni prāyaśo lauhāni bhavanti; tatpratirūpakāṇi
                        vā tadalābhe ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.8"><l> tatra, nānāprakārāṇāṃ vyālānāṃ mṛgapakṣiṇāṃ
                        mukhair mukhāni yantrāṇāṃ prāyaśaḥ sadṛśāni; tasmāt
                        tatsārūpyādāgamādupadeśādanyayantradarśanādyuktitaś ca kārayet
                        ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.9"><l> samāhitāni yantrāṇi kharaślakṣṇamukhāni ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         sudṛḍhāni surūpāṇi sugrahāṇi ca kārayet ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.10"><l> tatra svastikayantrāṇi- aṣṭādaśāṅgulapramāṇāni,
                        siṃhavyāghravṛkatarakṣvṛkṣadvīpimārjāraśṛgālamṛgair
                        vārukakākakaṅkakuraracāsabhāsaśaśaghātyulūkacilliśyena-
                        gṛdhrakrauñcabhṛṅgarājāñjalikarṇāvabhañjananandīmukhamukhāni ,
                        masūrākṛtibhiḥ kīlair avabaddhāni , mūle 'ṅkuśavadāvṛttavāraṅgāṇi
                        , asthividaṣṭaśalyoddharaṇārtham upadiśyante ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="118" n="32a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.11"><l> sanigraho 'nigrahaś ca sandaṃśau ṣoḍaśāṅgulau
                        bhavataḥ, tvaṅmāṃsasirāsnāyugataśalyoddharaṇārtham upadiśyete
                        ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.12"><l> tālayantre - dvādaśāṅgule
                        matsyatālavadekatāladvitālake, karṇanāsānāḍīśalyānāmāharaṇārtham
                        ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="118" n="32b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.13"><l> nāḍīyantrāṇi - anekaprakārāṇi,
                        anekaprayojanāni, ekatomukhānyubhayatomukhāni ca; tāni
                        srotogataśalyoddharaṇārthaṃ, rogadarśanārtham, ācūṣaṇārthaṃ,
                        kriyāsaukaryārthaṃ ceti; tāni srotodvārapariṇāhāni,
                        yathāyogadīrghāṇi ca | tatra
                        bhagandarārśovraṇabastyuttarabastimūtravṛddhidakodaradhūmaniruddhaprakaśasanniruddhagudayantrāṇyalābūśṛṅgayantrāṇi
                        copariṣṭādvakṣyāmaḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="119" n="33b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.14"><l> śalākāyantrāṇyapi nānāprakārāṇi,
                        nānāprayojanāni, yathāyogapariṇāhadīrghāṇi ca; teṣāṃ
                        gaṇḍūpadasarpaphaṇaśarapuṅkhabaḍiśamukhe dve dve,
                        eṣaṇavyūhanacālanāharaṇārtham upadiśyete ; masūradalamātramukhe
                        dve kiñcidānatāgre srotogataśalyoddharaṇārthaṃ; ṣaṭ
                        kārpāsakṛtoṣṇīṣāṇi , pramārjanakriyāsu; trīṇi darvyākṛtīni
                        khallamukhāni, kṣārauṣadhapraṇidhānārthaṃ ; trīṇyanyāni
                        jāmbavavadanāni, trīṇyaṅkuśavadanāni, ṣaḍevāgnikarmasvabhipretāni;
                        nāsārbudaharaṇārthamekaṃ kolāsthidalamātramukhaṃ
                        khallatīkṣṇauṣṭhaṃ ; añjanārthamekaṃ kalāyaparimaṇḍalamubhayato
                        mukulāgraṃ; mūtramārgaviśodhanārthamekaṃ
                        mālatīpuṣpavṛntāgrapramāṇaparimaṇḍalam iti ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="120" n="34b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.15"><l> upayantrāṇyapi-
                        rajjuveṇikāpaṭṭacarmāntavalkalalatāvastrāṣṭhīlāśmamudgarapāṇipādatalāṅgulijihvā-
                        dantanakhamukhabālāśvakaṭakaśākhāṣṭhīvanapravāhaṇaharṣāyaskāntamayāni
                        kṣārāgnibheṣajāni ceti ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.16"><l> etāni dehe sarvasmin dehasyāvayave tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         sandhau koṣṭhe dhamanyāṃ ca yathāyogaṃ prayojayet ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="121" n="35a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.17"><l> yantrakarmāṇi tu-
                        nirghātanapūraṇabandhanavyūhanavartanacālanavivartanavivaraṇapīḍanamārgaviśodhanavikarṣaṇāharaṇāñchanonnamana-
                        vinamanabhañjanonmathanācūṣaṇaiṣaṇadāraṇarjūkaraṇaprakṣālanapradhamanapramārjanāni
                        caturviṃśatiḥ ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.18"><l> svabuddhyā cāpi vibhajedyantrakarmāṇi buddhimān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         asaṅkhyeyavikalpatvāc chalyānām iti niścayaḥ ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.19"><l> tatra, atisthūlam, asāram, atidīrgham,
                        atihrasvam, agrāhi, viṣamagrāhi, vakraṃ, śithilam, atyunnatam,
                        mṛdukīlaṃ, mṛdumukhaṃ, mṛdupāśam, iti dvādaśa yantradoṣāḥ ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.20"><l> etair doṣair vinirmuktaṃ yantramaṣṭādaśāṅgulam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         praśastaṃ bhiṣajā jñeyaṃ taddhi karmasu yojayet ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.21"><l> dṛśyaṃ siṃhamukhādyais tu gūḍhaṃ
                        kaṅkamukhādibhiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nirhareta śanaiḥ śalyaṃ śāstrayuktivyapekṣayā ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.22"><l> ni(vi)vartate sādhvavagāhate ca śalyaṃ
                        nigṛhyoddharate ca yasmāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         yantreṣv ataḥ kaṅkamukhaṃ pradhānaṃ sthāneṣu sarveṣv adhi(vi)kāri
                        caiva ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.7.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        yantravidhirnāma saptamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||7|| </l></lg>





                </div>

                <div n="8" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.1"><l> athātaḥ śastrāvacāraṇīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.3"><l> viṃśatiḥ śastrāṇi, tad yathā- maṇḍalāgrakara<pb facs="122" n="36a"/>patravṛddhipatranakhaśastramudrikotpalapatrakārdhadhārasūcīkuśapatrāṭīmukha-
                        śarārimukhāntarmukhatrikūrcakakuṭhārikāvrīhimukhārāvetasapatrakabaḍiśadantaśaṅkveṣaṇya
                        iti ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="124" n="38b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.4"><l> tatra maṇḍalāgrakarapatre syātāṃ chedane lekhane
                        ca, vṛddhipatranakhaśastramudrikotpalapatrakārdhadhārāṇi chedane
                        bhedane ca,
                        sūcīkuśapatrāṭī(ṭā)mukhaśarārimukhāntarmukhatrikūrcakāni
                        visrāvaṇe, kuṭhārikāvrīhimukhārāvetasapatrakāṇi vyadhane sūcī ca,
                        baḍiśaṃ dantaśaṅkuś cāharaṇe, eṣaṇyeṣaṇe ānulomye ca, sūcyaḥ
                        sīvane; ity aṣṭavidhe karmaṇyupayogaḥ śastrāṇāṃ vyākhyātaḥ ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="125" n="39a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.5"><l> teṣāṃ atha yathāyogaṃ grahaṇasamāsopāyaḥ karmasu
                        vakṣyate- tatra vṛddhipatraṃ vṛntaphalasādhāraṇe bhāge gṛhṇīyāt,
                        bhedanānyevaṃ sarvāṇi, vṛddhipatraṃ maṇḍalāgraṃ ca kiñciduttānena
                        pāṇinā lekhane bahuśo 'vacāryaṃ, vṛntāgre visrāvaṇāni, viśeṣeṇa tu
                        bālavṛddhasukumārabhīrunārīṇāṃ rājñāṃ rājamā(pu)trāṇāṃ ca
                        trikūrcakena visrāvayet,
                        talapracchāditavṛntamaṅguṣṭhapradeśinībhyāṃ vrīhimukhaṃ,
                        kuṭhārikāṃ
                        vāmahastanyastāmitarahastamadhyamāṅgulyā'ṅguṣṭhaviṣṭabdhayā'bhihanyāt
                        , ārākarapatraiṣaṇyo mūle, śeṣāṇi tu yathāyogaṃ gṛhṇīyāt ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.6"><l> teṣāṃ nāmabhir evākṛtayaḥ prāyeṇa vyākhyātāḥ
                        ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.7-8"><l> tatra nakhaśastraiṣaṇyāvaṣṭāṅgule , sūcyo
                        vakṣyante, (pradeśinyagraparvapradeśapramāṇā mudrikā, daśāṅgulā
                        śarārimukhī sā ca (yā sā) kartarīti kathyate) | śeṣāṇi tu
                        ṣaḍaṅgulāni ||7||<pb facs="126" n="40a"/> tāni sugrahāṇi,
                        sulohāni, sudhārāṇi, surūpāṇi, susamāhitamukhāgrāṇi, akarālāni,
                        ceti śastrasampat ||8|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.9"><l> tatra vakraṃ, kuṇṭhaṃ, khaṇḍaṃ, kharadhāram,
                        atisthūlam, atyalpam, atidīrgham, atihrasvam, ity aṣṭau
                        śastradoṣāḥ | ato viparītaguṇam ādadīta, anyatra karapatrāt; tad
                        dhi kharadhāram asthicchedanārtham ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.10"><l> tatra dhārā bhedanānāṃ māsūrī, lekhanānām
                        ardhamāsūrī, vyadhanānāṃ visrāvaṇānāṃ ca kaiśikī, chedanānām
                        ardhakaiśikīti ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="126" n="40b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.11"><l> baḍiśaṃ dantaśaṅkuś cānatāgre |
                        tīkṣṇakaṇṭakaprathamayavapatramukhyeṣaṇī (gaṇḍūpadākāramukhī ca)
                        ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.12"><l> teṣāṃ pāyanā trividhā kṣārodakataileṣu | tatra
                        kṣārapāyitaṃ śaraśalyāsthicchedaneṣu, udakapāyitaṃ
                        māṃsacchedanabhedanapāṭaneṣu, tailapāyitaṃ
                        sirāvyadhanasnāyucchedaneṣu ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="127" n="41a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.13"><l> teṣāṃ niśānārthaṃ ślakṣṇaśilā māṣavarṇā;
                        dhārāsaṃsthāpanārthaṃ śālmalīphalakam iti ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.14"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         yadā suniśitaṃ śastraṃ romacchedi susaṃsthitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sugṛhītaṃ pramāṇena tadā karmasu yojayet ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.15"><l> anuśastrāṇi tu
                        tvaksārasphaṭikakācakuruvindajalaukognikṣāranakhagojīśephālikāśākapatrakarīrabālāṅgulaya
                        iti ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.16"><l> śiśūnāṃ śastrabhīrūṇāṃ śastrābhāve ca yojayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tvaksārādicaturvargaṃ chedye bhedye ca buddhimān ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.17"><l> āhāryacchedyabhedyeṣu nakhaṃ śakyeṣu yojayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         vidhiḥ pravakṣyate paścāt kṣāravahnijalaukasām ||17||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.18"><l> ye syur mukhagatā rogā netravartmagatāś ca ye |
                        <caesura/>
                         gojīśephālikāśākapatrairvisrāvayet tu tān ||18||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.19"><l> eṣyeṣv eṣaṇyalābhe tu bālāṅgulyaṅkurā hitāḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         śastrāṇy etāni matimān śuddhaśaikyāyasāni tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         kārayet karaṇaprāptaṃ karmāraṃ karmakovidam ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.20"><l> prayogajñasya vaidyasya siddhir bhavati nity
                        aśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmāt paricayaṃ kuryāc chastrāṇāṃ grahaṇe sadā ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.8.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        śastrāvacāraṇīyo nāmāṣṭamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||8|| </l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="9" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.1"><l> athāto yogyāsūtrīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="128" n="42a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.3"><l> adhigatasarvaśāstrārtham api śiṣyaṃ yogyāṃ
                        kārayet | snehādiṣu chedyādiṣu ca karmapatham upadiśet |
                        subahuśruto 'pyakṛtayogyaḥ karmasvayogyo bhavati ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.4"><l> tatra,
                        puṣpaphalālābūkālindakatrapusai(so)rvārukarkārukaprabhṛtiṣu
                        chedyaviśeṣān darśayet, utkartanāpakartanāni copadiśet;
                        dṛtibastiprasevakaprabhṛtiṣūdakapaṅkapūrṇeṣu bhedyayogyāṃ; saromṇi
                        carmaṇyātate lekhyasya; mṛtapaśusirāsūtpalanāleṣu ca vedhyasya;
                        ghuṇopahatakāṣṭhaveṇunalanālīśuṣkālābūmukheṣveṣyasya;
                        panasabimbībilvaphalamajjamṛtapaśudanteṣvāhāryasya;
                        madhūcchiṣṭopalipte śālmalīphalake visrāvyasya;
                        sūkṣmaghanavastrāntayor mṛducarmāntayoś ca sīvyasya;
                        pustamayapuruṣāṅgapratyaṅgaviśeṣeṣu bandhanayogyāṃ; mṛduṣu
                        māṃsakhaṇḍeṣv agnikṣārayogyāṃ; mṛducarmamāṃsapeśīṣūtpalanāleṣu ca
                        karṇasandhibandhayogyām;
                        udakapūrṇaghaṭapārśvasrotasyalābūmukhādiṣu ca
                        netrapraṇidhānabastivraṇabastipīḍanayogyām iti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.5ab"><l> bhavataś cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         evam ādiṣu medhāvī yogyārheṣu yathāvidhi |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.5cd"><l> dravyeṣu yogyāṃ kurvāṇo na pramuhyati karmasu
                        ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.6ab"><l> tasmāt kauśalam anvicchan
                        śastrakṣārāgnikarmasu |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.6cd"><l> yasya yatreha sādharmyaṃ tatra yogyāṃ
                        samācaret ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.9.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        yogyāsūtrīyo nāma navamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||9|| </l></lg>
                </div>


                <div n="10" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.1"><l> athāto viśikhānupraveśanīyam adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.3"><l> adhigatatantreṇopāsitatantrārthena dṛṣṭakarmaṇā
                        kṛtayogyena śāstraṃ nigadatā rājānujñātena nīcanakharomṇā śucinā
                        śuklavastraparihitena chatravatā daṇḍahastena
                        sopānatkenānuddhataveśena sumanasā kalyāṇābhivyāhāreṇākuhakena
                        bandhubhūtena bhūtānāṃ susahāyavatā vaidyena viśikhā'nupraveṣṭavyā
                        ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="129" n="43a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.4.1"><l> tato
                        dūtanimittaśakunamaṅgalānulomyenāturagṛhamabhigamya, upaviśya,
                        āturamabhipaśyet spṛśet pṛcchec ca | tribhir etairvijñānopāyai
                        rogāḥ prāyaśo veditavyā </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.4.2"><l> ity eke; tat tu na samyak, ṣaḍvidho hi
                        rogāṇāṃ vijñānopāyaḥ, tad yathā- pañcabhiḥ śrotrādibhiḥ praśnena
                        ceti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.5"><l> tatra śrotrendriyavijñeyā viśeṣā rogeṣu
                        vraṇāsrāvavijñānīyādiṣu vakṣyante- ‘tatra saphenaṃ

                        sparśanendriyavijñeyāḥ śītoṣṇaślakṣṇakarkaśamṛdukaṭhinatvād ayaḥ
                        (sparśaviśeṣā ) jvaraśophādiṣu, cakṣurindriyavijñeyāḥ
                        śarīropacayāpacayāyurlakṣaṇabalavarṇavikārādayaḥ,
                        rasanendriyavijñeyāḥ pramehādiṣu rasaviśeṣāḥ, ghrāṇendriyavijñeyā
                        ariṣṭaliṅgādiṣu vraṇānāmavraṇānāṃ ca gandhaviśeṣāḥ, praśnena ca

                        vedanāsamucchrāyaṃ balamantaragniṃ vātamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ
                        pravṛttimapravṛttiṃ kālaprakarṣādīṃś ca viśeṣān | ātmasadṛśeṣu
                        vijñānābhyupāyeṣu tatsthānīyair jānīyāt ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.6"><l> evam abhisamīkṣya sādhyān sādhayet, yāpyān
                        yāpayet, asādhyānnaivopakrameta, parisaṃvatsarotthitāṃś ca vikārān
                        prāyaśo varjayet ||6|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.7"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>

                        śrotriyanṛpatistrībālavṛddhabhīrurājasevakakitavadurbalavaidyavidagdhavyādhigopakadaridrakṛpaṇakrodhanānāmanātmavatāmanāthānāṃ
                        ca; evaṃ nirūpya cikitsāṃ kurvan dharmārthakāmayaśāṃsi prāpnoti

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.10.9"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>

                <div n="11" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.11.5"><l> nānauṣadhisamavāyāt tridoṣaghnaḥ , śuklatvāt
                            <pb facs="130" n="44a"/>saumyaḥ; tasya saumyasyāpi sato

                        vistaro 'nyatra ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.11.11"><l> athetaras trividho mṛdurmadhyastīkṣṇaś ca |

                        ādīpayet | athopaśānte 'gnau tadbhasma

                        kṣāradroṇam udakadroṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhir āloḍya mūtrair vā yathoktair

                        kumbhe saṃvṛtamukhe nidadhyād eṣa madhyamaḥ || 11 ||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="137" n="51a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.3"><l> kṣārādagnirgarīyān kriyāsu vyākhyātaḥ,
                        taddagdhānāṃ rogāṇām apunarbhāvād bheṣaja<pc> </pc>śastrakṣārair
                        asādhyānāṃ tatsādhyatvāc ca ||3||</l></lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.4"><l> athemāni dahanopakaraṇāni bhavanti- tad yathā-
                            pippalyajāśakṛdgodantaśaraśalākā<pc> </pc>jāmbavauṣṭhetaralauhāḥ kṣaudraguḍasnehāś ca | tatra,
                        pippalyajāśakṛdgodantaśaraśalākās tvaggatānāṃ,
                        jāmbavauṣṭhetaralauhā māṃsagatānāṃ, kṣaudraguḍasnehāḥ
                        sirāsnāyusandhyasthigatānām ||4|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.5"><l> tatrāgnikarma sarvartuṣu kuryād anyatra
                        śaradgrīṣmābhyāṃ; tatrāpyātyayike 'gnikarmasādhye vyādhau
                        tatpratyanīkaṃ vidhiṃ kṛtvā ||5|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.6"><l> sarvavyādhiṣvṛtuṣu ca picchilamannaṃ
                        bhuktavataḥ; mūḍhagarbhāśmarībhagandarodarārśomukharogeṣv
                        abhuktavataḥ karma kurvīta ||6|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.7"><l> tatra dvividhamagnikarmāhureke- tvagdagdhaṃ,
                            māṃsa<pb facs="138" n="52a"/>dagdhaṃ ca; iha tu
                            sirāsnāyu<pc> </pc>sandhyasthiṣv api na pratiṣiddho 'gniḥ
                        ||7|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.8"><l> tatra, śabdaprādurbhāvo durgandhatā
                        tvaksaṅkocaś ca tvagdagdhe, kapotavarṇatā'lpaśvayathuvedanā
                        śuṣkasaṅkucitavraṇatā ca māṃsadagdhe; kṛṣṇonnatavraṇatā
                        srāvasannirodhaś ca sirāsnāyudagdhe, rūkṣāruṇatā
                        karkaśasthiravraṇatā ca sandhyasthidagdhe ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <p xml:id="NS.1.12.8b" style="stream1">
                        
                         sthiravraṇatā kaṭhinavraṇatā ||8||</p>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.9"><l> tatra śirorogādhimanthayor
                        bhrūlalāṭaśaṅkhapradeśeṣu dahet, vartmarogeṣv
                        ārdrālaktakapraticchannāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ kṛtvā vartmaromakūpān ||9|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.10"><l> tvaṅmāṃsa<pc> </pc>sirāsnāyusandhyasthisthite
                        'tyugraruji vāyāv ucchritakaṭhinasuptamāṃse vraṇe granthyarśo
                        'rbudabhagandarāpacīślīpadacarmakīlatilakālakāntravṛddhisandhisirācchedanādiṣu
                        nāḍīśoṇitātipravṛttiṣu cāgnikarma kuryāt ||10|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.11"><l> tatra valaya-bindu-vilekhā-pratisāraṇānīti
                        dahanaviśeṣāḥ ||11|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.12"><l> bhavati cātra —
                        <caesura/>
                         rogasya saṃsthānam avekṣya samyaṅ narasya marmāṇi balābalaṃ ca ||
                        <caesura/>
                         vyādhiṃ tathartuṃ ca samīkṣya samyak tato 'vyavasyed bhiṣag
                        agnikarma ||12|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.13"><l> tatra samyagdagdhe madhusarpirbhyām abhyaṅgaḥ
                        ||13|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.14"><l> athemānagninā pariharet — pittaprakṛtim
                        antaḥśoṇitaṃ bhinnakoṣṭhamanuddhṛtaśalyaṃ durbalaṃ bālaṃ vṛddhaṃ
                            bhīrumaneka<pc> </pc>vraṇa<pc> </pc>pīḍitamasvedyāṃś ceti
                        ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.15"><l> ata ūrdhvamitarathādagdhalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ |
                        tatra, snigdhaṃ rūkṣaṃ vā''(cā)śritya dravyamagnir dahati;
                        agnisantapto hi snehaḥ sūkṣmasirānusāritvāt tvagādīnanupraviśyāśu
                        dahati; tasmāt snehadagdhe 'dhikā rujo bhavanti ||15|| </l></lg>


                    <pb facs="139" n="53a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.16"><l> tatra pluṣṭaṃ durdagdhaṃ
                        samyagdagdhamatidagdhaṃ ceti caturvidhamagnidagdham | tatra yad
                        vivarṇaṃ pluṣyate 'timātraṃ tat pluṣṭaṃ; yatrottiṣṭhanti sphoṭās
                        tīvrāś coṣadāharāgapākavedanāś cirāc copaśāmyanti tad durdagdhaṃ;
                        samyagdagdham anavagāḍhaṃ tālavarṇaṃ susaṃsthitaṃ
                        pūrvalakṣaṇayuktaṃ ca; atidagdhe māṃsāvalambanaṃ gātraviśleṣaḥ
                        sirāsnāyusandhyasthivyāpādanamatimātraṃ
                        jvaradāhapipāsāmūrcchāścopadravā bhavanti, vraṇaś cāsya cireṇa
                        rohati, rūḍhaś ca vivarṇo bhavati | tad etac caturvidham
                        agnidagdhalakṣaṇam ātmakarmaprasādhakaṃ bhavati ||16|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.12.17"><l> bhavanti cātra - agninā kopitaṃ raktaṃ bhṛśaṃ
                        jantoḥ prakupyati |
                        <caesura/>
                         tatas tenaiva vegena pittamasyābhyudīryate ||17|| </l></lg>

                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>
