                <div n="13" type="adhyāya">

                    <head>trayodaśo 'dhyāyaḥ |</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.1"><l> athāto jalaukāvacāraṇīyam adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || 1 || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || 2 || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.3"><l> nṛpāḍhya<pc> </pc>bāla<pc> </pc>sthavira<pc> </pc>bhīru<pc> </pc>durbala<pc> </pc>nārī<pc> </pc>sukumārāṇām anugrahārthaṃ paramasukumāro
                        'yaṃ śoṇitāvasecanopāyo 'bhihito jalaukasaḥ || 3 || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.4"><l> tatra vātapittakaphaduṣṭaśoṇitaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ
                        śṛṅgajalaukālābubhir avasecayet sarvāṇi sarvair vā (viśeṣas tu
                        visrāvyaṃ śṛṅgajalaukālābubhir gṛhṇīyāt) ||4 || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.5"><l>bhavanti cātra ślokāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         uṣṇaṃ samadhuraṃ snigdhaṃ gavāṃ śṛṅgaṃ prakīrtitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmād vātopasṛṣṭe tu hitaṃ tad avasecane || 5 ||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.6"><l> śītādhivāsā madhurā jalaukā vārisaṃbhavā
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmāt pittopasṛṣṭe tu hitā sā tv avasecane || 6 || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.7"><l> alābu kaṭukaṃ rūkṣaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca parikīrtitam ||
                        tasmāc chleṣmopasṛṣṭe tu hitaṃ tad avasecane || 7 || </l></lg>

                    <!-- -->
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.8"><l> tatra pracchite tanu<pc> </pc>basti<pc> </pc>paṭalāvanaddhena śṛṅgeṇa śoṇitam avasecaye, ācūṣaṇāt,
                        sāntardīpayā'labdhā | (jalāyukā vakṣyante) || 8 || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.9"><l> jalam āsām āyur ity jalāyukāḥ, jalam āsām oka
                        ity jalaukasaḥ || 9 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.10"><l> tā dvādaśa; tāsāṃ saviṣāḥ ṣaṭ, tāvatya eva
                        nirviṣāḥ || 10 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.11"><l>tatra saviṣāḥ - kṛṣṇā, karburā, alagardā,
                        indrāyudhā, sāmudrikā, govandanā ceti | tāsu, añjanacūrṇavarṇā
                        pṛthuśīrāḥ kṛṣṇā; varmimatsyavad āyatā cchinnonnatakukṣiḥ karburā;
                        | romaśā mahāpārśvā kṛṣṇamukhī alagardā; | indrāyudhavad
                        ūrddhvarājbhiś citrā indrāyudhā; | īṣadasitapītikā
                        vicitrapuṣpākṛticitrā sāmudrikāḥ; | govṛṣaṇavad adhobhāge
                        dvidhābhūtākṛtir aṇumukhī govandaneti | tābhir daṣṭe puruṣe daṃśe
                        śvayathur atimātraṃ kaṇḍūmūrcchā jvaro dāhaś chardir madaḥ sadanam
                        iti liṅgāni bhavanti | tatra mahāgadaḥ
                        pānālepananasykarmādiṣūpayojyaḥ | indrāyudhādaṣṭam asādhyam | ity
                        etāḥ saviṣāḥ sacikitsitā vyākhyātāḥ || 11 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.12"><l> atha nirviṣāḥ | kapilā, piṅgalā, śaṅkumukhī,
                        mūṣikā, puṇḍarīkamukhī, sāvarikā ceti | tatra
                        manaḥśilārañjitābhyām iva pārśvābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhe snigdhā mudgavarṇā
                        kapilā; kiñcidraktā vṛttakāyā piṅgā''śugā ca piṅgalā; yakṛdvarṇā
                        śīghrapāyinī dīrghatīkṣṇamukhī śaṅkumukhī;
                        mūṣikākṛtivarṇā'niṣṭagandhā ca mūṣikā; mudgavarṇā
                        puṇḍarīkatulyavaktrā puṇḍarīkamukhī; snigdhā
                        padmapatravarṇā'ṣṭādaśāṅgulapramāṇā sāvarikā, sā ca paśvarthe; ity
                        etā aviṣā vyākhyātāḥ || 12 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.13"><l> tāsāṃ yavanapāṇḍyasahya<pc> </pc>pautanādīni
                        kṣetrāṇi; teṣu mahāśarīrā balavatyaḥ śīghrapāyinyo mahāśanā
                        nirviṣāś ca viśeṣeṇa bhavanti || 13 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.14"><l> tatra,
                        saviṣamatsyakīṭadarduramūtrapurīṣakothajātāḥ kaluṣeṣv ambhasu ca
                        saviṣāḥ; padmotpala<pc> </pc>nalina<pc> </pc>kumuda<pc> </pc>saugandhika<pc> </pc>kuvalaya<pc> </pc>puṇḍarīka<pc> </pc>śaivala<pc> </pc>kotha<pc> </pc>jātā
                        vimaleṣv ambhaḥsu ca nirvviṣāḥ || 14 || </l></lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.15"><l>bhavanti cātra -
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣetreṣu vicaranty etāḥ salilāḍhyasugandhiṣu ||
                        <caesura/>
                         na ca saṃkīrṇacāriṇyo na ca paṅkeśayāḥ sukhāḥ 15 </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.16"><l> tāsāṃ grahaṇam ārdracarmaṇā, anyair vā
                        prayogair gṛhṇīyāt || 16 || </l></lg>
                    <!--                    -->
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.17"><l> athaināṃ nave mahati ghaṭe
                        sarastaḍāgodakapaṅkamāvāpya nidadhyāt; bhakṣyārthe cāsām upaharec
                        chaivalaṃ vallūram audakāṃś ca kandāṃś cūrṇīkṛtya; śayyārthaṃ
                        tṛṇam audakāni ca patrāṇi; tryahāt tryahāc cābhyo'nyajjalaṃ
                        bhakṣyaṃ ca dadyāt; saptarātrāt saptarātrāc ca ghaṭam anyaṃ
                        saṅkrāmayet ||17||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.18"><l> sthūlamadhyāḥ parikliṣṭāḥ pṛthvyo
                        mandaviceṣṭitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         agrāhiṇyo 'lpapāyinyaḥ saviṣāś ca na pūjitāḥ || 18 || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.19"><l> atha jalaukovasekasādhyavyādhitam upaveśya
                        saṃveśya vā, virūkṣya cāsya tam avakāśaṃ mṛdgomayacūrṇair yady
                        arujaḥ syāt | gṛhītāś ca tāḥ sarṣaparajanīkalkodakapradigdhagātrīḥ
                        salilasarakamadhye muhūrtasthitā vigataklamā jñātvā tābhī rogaṃ
                        grāhayet | ślakṣṇaśuklārdrapicuprotāvacchannāṃ kṛtvā mukham
                        apāvṛṇuyāt; agṛhṇantyai kṣīravinduṃ śoṇitabinduṃ vā dadyāt,
                        śastrapadāni vā kurvīta; yady evam api na gṛhṇīyāt tadā'nyāṃ
                        grāhayet ||19||</l></lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.20"><l>yadā ca niviśate 'śvakhuravad ānanaṃ
                        kṛtvonnamya ca skandhaṃ tadā jānīyād gṛhṇātīti; gṛhṇantīṃ
                        cārdravastrāvac channāṃ kṛtvā dhārayet ||20|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.21"><l>daṃśe todakaṇḍuprādurbhāvair jānīyāc chuddham
                        iyam ādatta iti; śuddham ādadānām apanayet; atha śoṇitagandhena na
                        muñcen mukham asyāḥ saindhavacūrṇenāvakiret ||21|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.22"><l>atha patitāṃ taṇḍulakaṇḍanapradigdhagātrīṃ
                        tailalavaṇābhyaktamukhīṃ vāmahastāṅguṣṭhāṅgulībhyāṃ gṛhītapucchāṃ
                        dakṣiṇahastāṅguṣṭhāṅgulibhyāṃ śanaiḥ śanair anulomam anumārjayed
                        āmukhāt, vāmayet tāvad yāvat samyagvāntaliṅgānīti | samyagvāntā
                        salilasarake nyastā bhoktukāmā satī caret | yā sīdatī na ceṣṭate
                        sā durvāntā, tāṃ punaḥ samyag vāmayet | durvāntāyā vyādhir asādhya
                        indramado nāma bhavati | atha suvāntāṃ pūrvavat sannidadhyāt
                        ||22|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.23"><l>śoṇitasya yogāyogānavekṣya
                        śatadhautaghṛtābhyaṅgaḥ, tatpicudhāraṇaṃ vā; jalaukovraṇān
                        madhunā'vaghaṭṭayet, śītābhir adbhiḥ pariṣecayed badhnīta vā,
                        kaṣāyamadhurasnigdhaśītaiś ca pradehaiḥ pradihyād iti ||23||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.24"><l> kṣetrāṇi grahaṇaṃ jātīḥ poṣaṇaṃ sāvacāraṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         jalaukasāṃ ca yo vetti tat sādhyān sa jayed gadān || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.13.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        jalaukāvacāraṇīyo nāma trayodaśo 'dhyāyaḥ || </l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="14" type="adhyāya">


                    <pb facs="145" n="59a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.1"><l> athātaḥ śoṇitavarṇanīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.3"><l> tatra pāñcabhautikasya caturvidhasya ṣaḍrasasya
                        dvividhavīryasyāṣṭavidhavīryasya vā'nekaguṇasyopayuktasyāhārasya
                        samyakpariṇatasya yas tejobhūtaḥ sāraḥ paramasūkṣmaḥ sa `rasaḥ’
                        ity ucyate, tasya hṛdayaṃ sthānaṃ sa hṛdayāc caturviṃśati dhamanīr
                        anupraviśyor dhvag ādaśa daśādhogāminyaś catasraś ca tiryaggāḥ
                        kṛtsnaṃ śarīram aharahas tarpayati vardhayati dhārayati yāpayati
                        cādṛṣṭahetukena karmaṇā | tasya śarīram anusarato 'numānād gatir
                        upalakṣayitavyā kṣayavṛddhivaikṛtaiḥ | tasmin
                        sarvaśarīrāvayavadoṣadhātumalāśayānusāriṇi rase jijñāsā kim ayaṃ
                        saumyas taijasa? iti | atrocyate sa khalu dravānusārī
                        snehanajīvanatarpaṇadhāraṇādibhir viśeṣaiḥ saumya ity avagamyate
                        ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.4"><l> sa khalv āpyo raso yakṛtplīhānau prāpya rāgam
                        upaiti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.5"><l> ślokau cātra bhavataḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         rañjitās tejasā tv āpaḥ śarīrasthena dehinām |
                        <caesura/>
                         avyāpannāḥ prasannena raktam ity abhidhīyate ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.6"><l> rasād eva striyā raktaṃ rajaḥsañjñaṃ pravartate
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         tad varṣād dvādaśād ūrdhvaṃ yāti pañcāśataḥ kṣayam ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.7"><l> ārtavaṃ śoṇitaṃ tv āgneyam agnīṣomīyatvād
                        garbhasya ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="146" n="60a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.8"><l> pāñcabhautikaṃ tv apare jīvaraktam āhur ācāryāḥ
                        ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.9"><l> visratā dravatā rāgaḥ spandanaṃ laghutā tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhūmyādīnāṃ guṇā hyete dṛśyante cātra śoṇite ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="147" n="61a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.10"><l> rasād raktaṃ tato māṃsaṃ māṃsān medaḥ
                        prajāyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         medaso 'sthi tato majjā majjñaḥ śukraṃ tu jāyate ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="148" n="63a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.11"><l> tatraiteṣāṃ dhātūnām annapānarasaḥ prīṇayitā
                        ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.12"><l> rasajaṃ puruṣaṃ vidyād rasaṃ rakṣet
                        prayatnataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         annātpānāñ ca matimān ācārāñ cāpy atandritaḥ ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="148" n="62b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.13"><l> tatra rasa gatau dhātuḥ aharahar gacchatīty
                        ato rasaḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.14"><l> sa khalu trīṇi trīṇi kalāsahasrāṇi pañcadaśa
                        ca kalā ekaikasmin dhātāv avatiṣṭhate evaṃ māsena rasaḥ śukraṃ
                        strīṇāṃ cārtavaṃ bhavati ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.15"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         aṣṭādaśasahasrāṇi saṅkhyā hy asmin samuccaye |
                        <caesura/>
                         kalānāṃ navatiḥ proktā svatantraparatantrayoḥ ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="149" n="63a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.16"><l> sa śabdārcir jalasantānavad aṇunā
                        viśeṣeṇānudhāvaty evaṃ śarīraṃ kevalam ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.17"><l> vājīkaraṇyas tv oṣadhayaḥ svabalaguṇotkarṣād
                        virecanavad upayuktāḥ śukraṃ śīghraṃ virecayanti ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.18"><l> yathā hi puṣpamukulastho gandho na śakyam
                        ihāstīti vaktuṃ, naiva nāstīti; atha cāsti, satāṃ bhāvānām
                        abhivyaktir iti jñātvā kevalaṃ saukṣmyān nābhivyajyate; sa eva
                        vivṛtapatrakeśare puṣpe kālāntareṇābhivyaktiṃ gacchati evaṃ
                        bālānām api vayaḥpariṇāmāc chukraprādurbhāvo bhavati,
                        romarājyādayaś ca viśeṣā nārīṇām ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="150" n="64a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.19"><l> sa evānnaraso vṛddhānāṃ (jarā)
                        paripakvaśarīratvād aprīṇano bhavati ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.20"><l> ta ete śarīradhāraṇād dhātava ity ucyante
                        ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.21"><l> teṣāṃ kṣayavṛddhī śoṇitanimitte, tasmāt tad
                        adhikṛtya vakṣyāmaḥ | tatra, phenilamaruṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ paruṣaṃ tanu
                        śīghragamaskandi ca vātena duṣṭaṃ; nīlaṃ pītaṃ haritaṃ śyāvaṃ
                        visramaniṣṭaṃ pipīlikāmakṣikāṇāmaskandi ca pittena duṣṭaṃ;
                        gairikodakapratīkāśaṃ snigdhaṃ śītalaṃ bahalaṃ picchilaṃ cirasrāvi
                        māṃsapeśīprabhaṃ ca śleṣmaduṣṭaṃ; sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ
                        kāñjikābhaṃ viśeṣato durgandhi ca sannipātaduṣṭaṃ; dvidoṣaliṅgaṃ
                        saṃsṛṣṭam ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.22"><l> indragopakapratīkāśamasaṃhatamavivarṇaṃ ca
                        prakṛtisthaṃ jānīyāt ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.23"><l> visrāvyāṇyanyatra vakṣyāmaḥ ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.24"><l> athāvisrāvyāḥ sarvāṅgaśophaḥ, kṣīṇasya
                        cāmlabhojananimittaḥ, pāṇḍurogyarśasodariśoṣigarbhiṇīnāṃ ca
                        śvayathavaḥ ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.25"><l> śastravisrāvaṇaṃ dvividhaṃ pracchānaṃ
                        sirāvyadhanaṃ ca ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="151" n="65a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.26"><l> tatra ṛjvasaṅkīrṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ samam anavagāḍham
                        anuttānam āśu ca śastraṃ pātayen marmasirāsnāyusandhīnāṃ
                        cānupaghāti ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.27"><l> tatra durdine durviddhe śītavātayor asvinne
                        bhuktamātre skandatvāc choṇitaṃ na sravaty alpaṃ vā sravati ||27|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.28"><l> madamūrcchāśramārtānāṃ vātaviṇmūtrasaṅginām |
                        <caesura/>
                         nidrābhibhūtabhītānāṃ nṛṇāṃ nāsṛk pravartate ||28|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.29"><l> tad duṣṭaṃ śoṇitamanirhriyamāṇaṃ
                        śophadāharāgapākavedanā janayet ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.30"><l> atyuṣṇe 'tisvinne 'tividdhe 'jñair
                        visrāvitamatipravartate ; tad atipravṛttaṃ śiro
                        'bhitāpamāndhyamadhimanthatimiraprādurbhāvaṃ dhātukṣayamākṣepakaṃ
                        dāhaṃ pakṣāghātamekāṅgavikāraṃ hikkāṃ śvāsakāsau pāṇḍurogaṃ
                        maraṇaṃ cāpādayati ||30|| </l></lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.31"><l> tasmānna śīte nātyuṣṇe nāsvinne nātitāpite |
                        <caesura/>
                         yavāgūṃ pratipītasya śoṇitaṃ mokṣayed bhiṣak ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.32"><l> samyag gatvā yadā raktaṃ svayam evāvatiṣṭhate
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śuddhaṃ tadā vijānīyāt samyagvisrāvitaṃ ca tat ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.33"><l> lāghavaṃ vedanāśāntir vyādher vegaparikṣayaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         samyagvisrāvite liṅgaṃ prasādo manasas tathā ||33|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.34"><l> tvagdoṣā granthayaḥ śophā rogāḥ śoṇitajāś ca
                        ye |
                        <caesura/>
                         raktamokṣaṇaśīlānāṃ na bhavanti kadācana ||34|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.35"><l> atha khalv apravartamāne rakte
                        elāśītaśivakuṣṭhatagarapāṭhābhadradāruviḍaṅgacitrakatrikaṭukāgāradhūmaharidrārkāṅkuranaktamālaphalair
                        yathālābhaṃ tribhiś caturbhiḥ samastair vā cūrṇīkṛtair
                        lavaṇatailapragāḍhair vraṇamukham avagharṣayet, evaṃ samyak
                        pravartate ||35|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.36"><l> athātipravṛtte
                        rodhramadhukapriyaṅgupattaṅgupattaṅgagairikasarjarasarasāñjanaśālmalīpuṣpaśaṅkhaśuktimāṣayavagodhūmacūrṇaiḥ
                        śanaiḥ śanair vraṇamukham avacūrṇyāṅgulyagreṇāvapīḍayet
                        sālasarjārjunārimedameṣaśṛṅgadhavadhanvanatvagbhir vā cūrṇitābhiḥ
                        kṣaumeṇa vā dhmāpitena samudraphenalākṣācūrṇair vā
                        yathoktarvraṇabandhanadravyair gāḍhaṃ badhnīyāt
                        śītācchādanabhojanāgāraiḥ śītaiḥ pradehapariṣekaiś copacaret,
                        kṣāreṇāgninā vā dahedyathoktaṃ, vyadhādanantaraṃ tāmevātipravṛttāṃ
                        sirāṃ vidhyet kākolyādikvāthaṃ vā śarkarāmadhumadhuraṃ pāyayet
                        eṇahariṇorabhraśaśamahiṣavarāhāṇāṃ vā rudhiraṃ kṣīrayūṣarasaiḥ
                        susnigdhaiś cāśnīyāt upadravāṃś ca yathāsvam upacaret ||36|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="152" n="36a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.37"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         dhātukṣayāt srute rakte mandaḥ sañjāyate 'nalaḥ | pavanaś ca
                        paraṃ kopaṃ yāti tasmāt prayatnataḥ ||37|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.38"><l> taṃ nātiśītair laghubhiḥ snigdhaiḥ
                        śoṇitavardhanaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         īṣad amlair anamlair vā bhojanaiḥ samupācaret ||38||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.39"><l> caturvidhaṃ yad etad dhi rudhirasya nivāraṇam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sandhānaṃ skandanaṃ caiva pācanaṃ dahanaṃ tathā ||39|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.40"><l> vraṇaṃ kaṣāyaḥ sandhatte raktaṃ skandayate
                        himam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tathā sampācayed bhasma dāhaḥ saṅkocayet sirāḥ ||40|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.41"><l> askandamāne rudhire sandhānāni prayojayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         sandhāne bhraśyamāne tu pācanaiḥ samupācaret ||41|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.42"><l> kalpair etais tribhir vaidyaḥ prayateta
                        yathāvidhi |
                        <caesura/>
                         asiddhimatsu caiteṣu dāhaḥ parama iṣyate ||42|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.43"><l> śeṣadoṣe yato rakte na vyādhirativartate |
                        <caesura/>
                         sāvaśeṣe tataḥ stheyaṃ na tu kuryād atikramam ||43|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.44"><l> dehasya rudhiraṃ mūlaṃ rudhireṇaiva dhāryate |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmād yatnena saṃrakṣyaṃ raktaṃ jīva iti sthitiḥ ||44|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.14.45"><l> srutaraktasya sekādyaiḥ śītaiḥ prakupite 'nile
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śophaṃ satodaṃ koṣṇena sarpiṣā pariṣecat ||45|| </l></lg>

                    <p ana="trailer">iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne śoṇitavarṇanīyo
                        nāma caturdaśo 'dhyāya ||14|| </p>

                <div n="15" type="adhyāya">


                    <pb facs="153" n="67a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.1"><l> athāto doṣadhātumalakṣayavṛddhivijñānīyam
                        adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.3"><l> doṣadhātumalamūlaṃ hi śarīraṃ, tasmād eteṣāṃ
                        lakṣaṇamucyamānam upadhāraya ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.4.1"><l> tatra
                        praspandanodvahanapūraṇavivekadhāraṇalakṣaṇo vāyuḥ pañcadhā
                        pravibhaktaḥ śarīraṃ dhārayati (1) |4|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.4.2"><l> rāgapaktyojastejomedhoṣmakṛt pittaṃ pañcadhā
                        pravibhaktam agnikarmaṇā'nugrahaṃ karoti; (2) |4|</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.4.3"><l>
                        sandhisaṃśleṣaṇasnehanaropaṇapūraṇabalasthairyakṛcchleṣmā pañcadhā
                        pravibhakta udakakarmaṇā'nugrahaṃ karoti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.5.1"><l> rasastuṣṭiṃ prīṇanaṃ raktapuṣṭiṃ ca karoti,
                        raktaṃ varṇaprasādaṃ<pb facs="154" n="68a"/> māṃsapuṣṭiṃ jīvayati
                        ca, māṃsaṃ śarīrapuṣṭiṃ medasaś ca, medaḥ snehasvedau dṛḍhatvaṃ
                        puṣṭimasthnāṃ ca, asthīni dehadhāraṇaṃ majjñaḥ puṣṭiṃ ca, majjā
                        snehaṃ balaṃ śukrapuṣṭiṃ pūraṇamasthnāṃ ca karoti, śukraṃ dhairyaṃ
                        cyavanaṃ prītiṃ dehabalaṃ harṣaṃ bījārthaṃ ca; (1) </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.5.2"><l> purīṣam upastambhaṃ vāyvagnidhāraṇaṃ ca,
                        bastipūraṇavikledakṛnmūtraṃ, svedaḥ kledatvaksaukumāryakṛt; (2) </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.5.3"><l> raktalakṣaṇamārtavaṃ garbhakṛcca, garbho
                        garbhalakṣaṇaṃ, stanyaṃ stanayor āpīnatvajananaṃ jīvanaṃ ceti
                        ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.6"><l>
                        <pb facs="154" n="68b"/>tatra vidhivat parirakṣaṇaṃ kurvīta ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.7"><l> ata ūrdhvam eṣāṃ kṣīṇalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ |
                        tatra, vātakṣaye mandaceṣṭatā'lpavāktvamapraharṣo mūḍhasañjñatā
                        ca, pittakṣaye mandoṣmāgnitā niṣprabhatā ca, śleṣmakṣaye
                        rūkṣatā'ntardāha āmāśayetaraśleṣmāśayaśūnyatā sandhiśaithilyaṃ
                        (tṛṣṇā daurbalyaṃ prajāgaraṇaṃ) ca ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.8"><l> tatra svayonivardhanānyeva pratīkāraḥ ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="155" n="69a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.9"><l> rasakṣaye hṛtpīḍākampaśūnyatāstṛṣṇā ca,
                        śoṇitakṣaye tvakpāruṣyamamlaśītaprārthanā sirāśaithilyaṃ ca,
                        māṃsakṣaye
                        sphiggaṇḍauṣṭhopasthoruvakṣaḥkakṣāpiṇḍikodaragrīvāśuṣkatā
                        raukṣyatodau gātrāṇāṃ sadanaṃ dhamanīśaithilyaṃ ca, medaḥkṣaye
                        plīhābhivṛddhiḥ sandhiśūnyatā raukṣyaṃ meduramāṃsaprārthanā ca,
                        asthikṣaye 'sthiśūlaṃ dantanakhabhaṅgo raukṣyaṃ ca, majjakṣaye
                        'lpaśukratā parvabhedo 'sthinistodo 'sthiśūnyatā ca, śukrakṣaye
                        meḍhravṛṣaṇavedanā'śaktirmaithune cirād vā prasekaḥ praseke
                        cālparaktaśukradarśanam ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.10"><l> tatrāpi svayonivardhanadravyopayogaḥ
                        (pratīkāraḥ ) ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.11"><l> purīṣakṣaye hṛdayapārśvapīḍā saśabdasya ca
                        vāyor ūrdhvagamanaṃ kukṣau sañcaraṇaṃ ca, mūtrakṣaye bastitodo
                        'lpamūtratā ca; atrāpi svayonivardhanadravyopayogaḥ | svedakṣaye
                        stabdharomakūpatā tvakśoṣaḥ sparśavaiguṇyaṃ svedanāśaśca;
                        tatrābhyaṅgaḥ svedopayogaś ca ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="156" n="70a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.12"><l> ārtavakṣaye yathocitakālādarśanamalpatā vā
                        yonivedanā ca; tatra saṃśodhanamāgneyānāṃ ca dravyāṇāṃ
                        vidhivadupayogaḥ | stanakṣaye stanayor mlānatā stanyāsambhavo
                        'lpatā vā; tatra śleṣmavardhanadravyopayogaḥ | garbhakṣaye
                        garbhāspandanamanunnatakukṣitā ca; tatra prāptabastikālāyāḥ
                        kṣīrabastiprayogo medyānnopayogaś ceti ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.13"><l> ata ūrdhvamativṛddhānāṃ doṣadhātumalānāṃ
                        lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | vṛddhiḥ punareṣāṃ
                        svayonivardhanātyupasevanādbhavati | tatra, vātavṛddhau
                        vākpāruṣyaṃ kārśyaṃ kārṣṇyaṃ gātrasphuraṇamuṣṇakāmi(ma)tā
                        nidrānāśo 'lpabalatvaṃ gāḍhavarcastvaṃ ca; pittavṛddhau
                        pītāvabhāsatā santāpaḥ śītakāmitvamalpanidratā mūrcchā
                        balahānirindriyadaurbalyaṃ pītaviṇmūtranetratvaṃ ca; śleṣmavṛddhau
                        śauklyaṃ śaity aṃ sthairyaṃ gauravamavasādastandrā nidrā
                        sandhiviśleṣaś ca ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="156" n="70b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.14"><l> raso 'tivṛddho hṛdayotkledaṃ prasekaṃ
                        cāpādayati; raktaṃ raktāṅgākṣitāṃ sirāpūrṇatvaṃ ca; māṃsaṃ
                        sphiggaṇḍauṣṭhopasthorubāhujaṅghāsu vṛddhiṃ gurugātratāṃ ca;
                        medaḥsnigdhāṅgatāmudarapārśvavṛddhiṃ kāsaśvāsādīn daurgandhyaṃ ca;
                        asthyadhyasthīnyadhidantāṃśca; majjā sarvāṅganetragauravaṃ ca;
                        śukraṃ śukrāśmarīmatiprādurbhāvaṃ ca ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.15"><l> purīṣam āṭopaṃ kukṣau śūlaṃ ca; mūtraṃ
                        mūtravṛddhiṃ muhur muhuḥ pravṛttiṃ bastitodam ādhmānaṃ ca; svedas
                        tvaco daurgandhyaṃ kaṇḍūṃ ca ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.16"><l> ārtavam aṅgamardam atipravṛttiṃ daurgandhyaṃ
                        ca; stanyaṃ stanayor āpīnatvaṃ muhurmuhuḥ pravṛttiṃ todaṃ ca;
                        garbho jaṭharābhivṛddhiṃ svedaṃ ca ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.17"><l> teṣāṃ yathāsvaṃ saṃśodhanaṃ kṣapaṇaṃ ca kṣayād
                        aviruddhaiḥ kriyāviśeṣaiḥ prakurvīta ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="157" n="71a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.18"><l> pūrvaḥ pūrvo 'tivṛddhatvād vardhayed dhi paraṃ
                        param |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmād atipravṛddhānāṃ dhātūnāṃ hrāsanaṃ hitam ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.19"><l> balalakṣaṇaṃ balakṣayalakṣaṇaṃ cāta ūrdhvam
                        upadekṣyāmaḥ | tatra rasādīnāṃ śukrāntānāṃ dhātūnāṃ yat paraṃ
                        tejastat khalvojastad eva balam ity ucyate, svaśāstrasiddhāntāt
                        ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.20"><l> tatra balena sthiropacitamāṃsatā
                        sarvaceṣṭāsvapratighātaḥ svaravarṇaprasādo bāhyānāmābhyantarāṇāṃ
                        ca karaṇānāmātmakāryapratipattir bhavati ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.21"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         ojaḥ somātmakaṃ snigdhaṃ śuklaṃ śītaṃ sthiraṃ saram |
                        <caesura/>
                         viviktaṃ mṛdu mṛtsnaṃ ca prāṇāyatanam uttamam ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.22ab"><l> dehaḥ sāvayavastena vyāpto bhavati dehinaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.22cd"><l> tad abhāvāc ca śīryante śarīrāṇi śarīriṇām
                        ||22||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.24"><l> tasya visraṃso vyāpat kṣaya iti (trayo doṣāḥ;)

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.32"><l> rasanimittam eva sthaulyaṃ kārśyaṃ ca | tatra
                        śleṣmalāhārasevino 'dhyaśanaśīlasyāvyāyāmino divāsvapnaratasya
                        cāma evānnaraso madhurataraś ca śarīramanukrāmannatisnehānmedo
                        janayati, tadatisthaulyamāpādayati ; tamatisthūlaṃ
                        kṣudraśvāsapipāsākṣutsvapnasvedagātradaurgandhyakrathanagātrasādagadgadatvāni
                        kṣipramevāviśanti, saukumāryānmedasaḥ sarvakriyāsvasamarthaḥ,
                        kaphamedoniruddhamārgatvāccālpavyavāyo bhavati, āvṛtamārgatvād eva
                        śeṣā dhātavo nāpyāyante 'tyarthamato 'lpaprāṇo bhavati,
                        pramehapiḍakājvarabhagandaravidradhivātavikārāṇāmanyatamaṃ prāpya
                        pañcatvam upayāti, sarva eva cāsya rogā balavanto
                        bhavantyāvṛtamārgatvāt srotasām; atas tasyotpattihetuṃ pariharet |
                        utpanne tu
                        śilājatuguggulugomūtratriphalāloharajorasāñjanamadhuyavamudgakoradūṣakaśyāmākoddālakādīnāṃ

                        lekhanabastyupayogaś ceti ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="160" n="74a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.15.33"><l> tatra punar vātalāhārasevino
                        'tivyāyāmavyavāyādhyayanabhayaśokadhyānarātrijāgaraṇapipāsākṣu

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.3"><l> rakṣābhūṣaṇanimittaṃ bālasya karṇau vidhyete |
                        tau ṣaṣṭhe māsi saptame vā śuklapakṣe praśasteṣu
                        tithikaraṇamuhūrtanakṣatreṣu kṛtamaṅgalasvastivācanaṃ dhātryaṅke

                        chidra ādityakarāvabhāsite śanaiḥ śanair dakṣiṇahastena rju
                        vidhyet pratanukaṃ sūcyā bahalam ārayā pūrvaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kumārasya
                        vāmaṃ kumāryāḥ tataḥ picuvartiṃ praveśayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.10"><l> tatra samāsena pañcadaśakarṇabandhākṛtayaḥ |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.15"><l> ato 'nyatamaṃ bandhaṃ cikīrṣur
                        agropaharaṇīyoktopasaṃbhṛtasaṃbhāraṃ viśeṣataś cātropaharet

                        bhojanais tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         ya evaṃ vetti doṣāṇāṃ cikitsāṃ kartum arhati || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.6"><l> ata ūrdhvaṃ nāmaliṅgair vakṣye pālyām
                        upadravān |
                        <caesura/>
                         atpāṭakaś cotpuṭakaḥ śyāvaḥ kaṇḍūyuto bhṛśam || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.7"><l> avamanthaḥ sakaṇḍūko granthiko jambulas
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         srāvī ca dāhavāṃś caiva śṛṇveṣāṃ kramaśaḥ kriyām || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.8"><l> apāmārgaḥ sarjarasaḥ pāṭalālakucatvacau |
                        <caesura/>
                         utpāṭake pralepaḥ syāt tailam ebhiś ca pācayet || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.9"><l> śampākaśigrupūtīkān godhāmedo 'tha tadvasām
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vārāhaṃ gavyam aiṇeyaṃ pittaṃ sarpiś ca saṃsṛjet || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.10"><l> lepam utpuṭake dadyāt tailam ebhiś ca
                        sādhitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         gaurīṃ sugandhāṃ saśyāmām anantāṃ taṇḍulīyakam | </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.11"><l> śyāve pralepanaṃ dadyāt tailam ebhiś ca
                        sādhitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāṭhāṃ rasāñjanaṃ kṣaudraṃ tathā syād uṣṇakāñjikam || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.12"><l> dadyāl lepaṃ sakaṇḍūke tailam ebhiś ca
                        sādhitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         vraṇībhūtasya deyaṃ syād idaṃ tailaṃ vijānatā || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.13"><l> madhukakṣīrakākolījīvakādyair vipācitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         godhāvarāhasarpāṇāṃ vasāḥ syuḥ kṛtabṛṃhaṇe || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.14"><l> pralepanam idaṃ dadyād avasicyāvamanthake |
                        <caesura/>
                         prapauṇḍarīkaṃ madhukaṃ samaṅgāṃ dhavam eva ca || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.15"><l> tailam ebhiś ca saṃpakvaṃ śṛṇu kaṇḍūmataḥ
                        kriyām |
                        <caesura/>
                         sahadevā viśvadevā ajākṣīraṃ sasaindhavam |
                        <caesura/>
                         etair ālepanaṃ dadyāt tailam ebhiś ca sādhitam || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.16"><l> granthike guṭikāṃ pūrvaṃ srāvayed avapāṭya
                        tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         tataḥ saindhavacūrṇaṃ tu ghṛṣṭvā lepaṃ pradāpayet || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.17"><l> likhitvā tatsrutaṃ ghṛṣṭvā cūrṇair
                        lodhrasya jambule |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣīreṇa pratisāryainaṃ śuddhaṃ saṃropayet tataḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.18"><l> madhuparṇī madhūkaṃ ca ma madhukaṃ madhunā
                        saha |
                        <caesura/>
                         lepaḥ srāviṇi dātavyas tailam ebhiś ca sādhitam || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.26.19"><l> pañcavalkaiḥ samadhukaiḥ piṣṭais taiś ca
                        ghṛtānvitaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         jīvakādyaiḥ sasarpiṣkair dahyamānaṃ pralepayet ||) </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.27"><l> viśleṣitāyās tv atha nāsikāyā
                        <caesura/>
                         vakṣyāmi sandhānavidhiṃ yathāvat |
                        <caesura/>
                         nāsāpramāṇaṃ pṛthivīruhāṇāṃ
                        <caesura/>
                         patraṃ gṛhītvā tv avalambi tasya || </l></lg>


                    <pb facs="165" n="81a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.28"><l> tena pramāṇena hi gaṇḍapārśvād
                        <caesura/>
                         utkṛtya baddhaṃ tv atha nāsikāgram |
                        <caesura/>
                         vilikhya cāśu pratisaṃdadhīta
                        <caesura/>
                         tat sādhubandhair bhiṣag apramattaḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.29"><l> susaṃhitaṃ samyag ato yathāvan
                        <caesura/>
                         nāḍīdvayenābhisamīkṣya baddhvā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pronnamya cainām avacūrṇayet tu
                        <caesura/>
                         pataṅgayaṣṭīmadhukāñjanaiś ca || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.30"><l> saṃchādya samyak picunā sitena
                        <caesura/>
                         tailena siñced asakṛt tilānām |
                        <caesura/>
                         ghṛtaṃ ca pāyyaḥ sa naraḥ sujīrṇe
                        <caesura/>
                         snigdho virecyaḥ sa yathopadeśam || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.31"><l> rūḍhaṃ ca sandhānam upāgataṃ syāt
                        <caesura/>
                         tad ardhaśeṣaṃ tu punar nikṛntet |
                        <caesura/>
                         hīnāṃ punar vardhayituṃ yateta
                        <caesura/>
                         samāṃ ca kuryād ativṛddhamāṃsām || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.16.32"><l> nāḍīyogaṃ vinauṣṭhasya nāsāsandhānavad vidhim
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         ya evam eva jānīyāt sa rājñaḥ kartum arhati || </l></lg>

                    <trailer xml:id="SS.1.16.colophon">iti śrīsuśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ
                        sūtrasthāne karṇavyadhabandhavidhirnāma ṣoḍaśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||
                    </trailer>
                </div>

                <div n="17" type="adhyāya">


                    <pb facs="165" n="80b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.1"><l> athāta āmapakvaiṣaṇīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.3"><l> śophasamutthānā granthividradhyalajīprabhṛtayaḥ
                        prāyeṇa vyādhayo 'bhihitā anekākṛtayaḥ tair vilakṣaṇaḥ
                        pṛthurgrathitaḥ samo viṣamo vā tvaṅmāṃsasthāyī doṣasaṅghātaḥ
                        śarīraikadeśotthitaḥ śopha ity ucyate ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.4"><l> sa ṣaḍvidho
                        vātapittakaphaśoṇitasannipātāgantunimittaḥ | tasya
                        doṣarūpavyañjanair lakṣaṇāni vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | tatra vātaśophaḥ
                        kṛṣṇo 'ruṇo vā paruṣo mṛduranavasthitāstodādayaś cātra
                        vedanāviśeṣā bhavanti pittaśophaḥ pīto mṛduḥ sarakto vā
                        śīghrānusāryoṣādayaś cātra vedanāviśeṣā bhavanti; śleṣmaśvayathuḥ
                            pāṇḍuḥ<pb facs="166" n="82a"/> kaṭhinaḥ snigdhaḥ śīto snigdho
                        mandānusārī kaṇḍvādayaś cātra vedanāviśeṣā bhavanti
                        sarvavarṇavedanaḥ sannipātaśvayathuḥ pittavacchoṇitajo 'tikṛṣṇaśca
                        pittaraktalakṣaṇa āganturlohitāvabhāsaś ca ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.5"><l> sa yadā bāhyābhyantaraiḥ kriyāviśeṣair na
                        sambhāvitaḥ praśamayituṃ kriyāviparyayādbahutvād vā doṣāṇāṃ tadā
                        pākābhimukho bhavati | tasyāmasya pacyamānasya pakvasya ca
                        lakṣaṇamucyamānam upadhāraya- | tatra, mandoṣmatā tvaksavarṇatā
                        śītaśophatā sthairyaṃ mandavedanatā'lpaśophatā
                        cāmalakṣaṇamuddiṣṭaṃ; sūcibhir iva nistudyate, daśyata iva
                        pipīlikābhiḥ, tābhiś ca saṃsarpyata iva, chidyata iva śastreṇa,
                        bhidyata iva śaktibhiḥ, tāḍyata iva daṇḍena, pīḍyata iva pāṇinā,
                        ghaṭyata iva cāṅgulyā, dahyate pacyata iva cāgnikṣārābhyām,
                        oṣacoṣaparīdāhāś ca bhavanti, vṛścikaviddha iva ca
                        sthānāsanaśayaneṣu na śāntim upaiti, ādhmātabastirivātataś ca
                        śopho bhavati , tvagvaivarṇyaṃ śophābhivṛddhirjvaradāhapipāsā
                        bhaktāruciś ca pacyamānaliṅgaṃ; vedanopaśāntiḥ pāṇḍutā'lpaśophatā
                        valīprādurbhāvastvakparipuṭanaṃ nimnadarśanamaṅgulyā'vapīḍite
                        pratyunnamanaṃ, bastāvivodakasañcaraṇaṃ pūyasya
                        prapīḍayatyekamantamante cāvapīḍite, muhurmuhustodaḥ
                        kaṇḍūrunnatatā vyādherupadravaśāntirbhaktābhikāṅkṣā ca pakvaliṅgam
                        | kaphajeṣu tu rogeṣu gambhīragatitvād abhighātajeṣu vā keṣucid
                        asamastaṃ pakvalakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā pakvamapakvam iti manyamāno
                        bhiṣaṅmoham upaiti tatra hi tvaksavarṇatā śītaśophatā sthairyam
                        alparujatā'śmavac ca ghanatā na moham upeyād iti ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.6"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         āmaṃ vipacyamānaṃ ca samyak pakvaṃ ca yo bhiṣak|
                        <caesura/>
                         jānīyāt sa bhaved vaidyaḥ śeṣās taskaravṛttayaḥ ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="167" n="83a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.7"><l> vātād ṛte nāsti rujā na pākaḥ pittādṛte nāsti
                        kaphāc ca pūyaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmāt samastān paripākakāle pacanti śophāṃs traya eva doṣāḥ
                        ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.8"><l> kālāntareṇābhyuditaṃ tu pittaṃ kṛtvā vaśe
                        vātakaphau prasahya |
                        <caesura/>
                         pacaty ataḥ śoṇitam eva pāko mato 'pareṣāṃ viduṣāṃ dvitīyaḥ
                        ||8||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.9"><l> tatra, āmacchede
                        māṃsasirāsnāyusandhyasthivyāpādanam atimātraṃ śoṇitātipravṛttir
                        vedanāprādurbhāvo 'vadaraṇam anekopadravadarśanaṃ kṣatavidradhirvā
                        bhavati | sa yadā bhayamohābhyāṃ pakvam apy apakvam iti manyamānaś
                        ciram upekṣate vyādhiṃ vaidyas tadā gambhīrānugato dvāram
                        alabhamānaḥ pūyaḥ svam āśrayam avadāryotsaṅgaṃ mahāntam avakāśaṃ
                        kṛtvā nāḍīṃ janayitvā kṛcchrasādhyo bhavaty asādhyo veti ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.10"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         yaś chinatty āmam ajñānād yaś ca pakvam upekṣate |
                        <caesura/>
                         śvapacāv iva mantavyau tāv aniścitakāriṇau ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.11"><l> prāk śastrakarmaṇaś ceṣṭaṃ bhojayed āturaṃ
                        bhiṣak |
                        <caesura/>
                         madyapaṃ pāyayen madyaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ yo vedanā'sahaḥ ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.12"><l> na mūrcchaty annasaṃyogān mattaḥ śastraṃ na
                        budhyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmād avaśyaṃ bhoktavyaṃ rogeṣūkteṣu karmaṇi ||12||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.13"><l> prāṇo hy ābhyantaro nṝṇāṃ
                        bāhyaprāṇaguṇānvitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhārayaty avirodhena śarīraṃ pāñcabhautikam ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="168" n="84a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.14"><l> alpo mahān vā kriyayā vinā yaḥ samucchritaḥ
                        pākam upaiti śophaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viśālamūlo viṣamaṃ vidagdhaḥ sa kṛcchratāṃ yāty avagāḍhadoṣaḥ
                        ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.15"><l> ālepavisrāvaṇāśodhanais tu samyak prayuktair
                        yadi nopaśāmyet |
                        <caesura/>
                         pacyeta śīghraṃ samam alpamūlaḥ sa piṇḍitaś copari connataḥ syāt
                        ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.16"><l> kakṣaṃ samāsādya yathaiva vahnirvāyvīritaḥ
                        sandahati prasahya |
                        <caesura/>
                         tathaiva pūyo 'py aviniḥsṛto hi māṃsaṃ sirāḥ snāyu ca khādatīha
                        ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.17"><l> ādau vimlāpanaṃ kuryād dvitīyam avasecanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛtīyam upanāhaṃ tu caturthīṃ pāṭanakriyām ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.18"><l> pañcamaṃ śodhanaṃ kuryāt ṣaṣṭhaṃ ropaṇam
                        iṣyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         ete kramā vraṇasyoktāḥ saptamaṃ vaikṛtāpaham ||18||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        āmapakvaiṣaṇīyo nāma saptadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||17|| </l></lg>

                <div n="18" type="adhyāya">




                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.1"><l> athāto vraṇālepanabandhavidhim adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.3"><l> ālepa ādya upakramaḥ eṣa sarvaśophānāṃ sāmānyaḥ
                        pradhānatamaś ca taṃ ca pratirogaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ tato bandhaḥ
                        pradhānaṃ tena śuddhir vraṇaropaṇam asthisaṃdhisthairyaṃ ca || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.4"><l> tatra pratilomam ālimpet | <pb facs="169" n="85a"/>pratilome hi samyagauṣadhamavatiṣṭhate 'nupraviśati
                        romakūpān svedavāhibhiśca sirāmukhairvīryaṃ prāpnoti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.5"><l> na ca śuṣyamāṇamupekṣeta, anyatra pīḍayitavyāt
                        | śuṣko hy apārthako rukkaraś ca ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.6"><l> sa trividhaḥ pralepaḥ, pradeha, ālepaś ca |
                        pralepapradehayor antaraṃ - tatra pralepaḥ śītastanur aviśoṣī
                        viśoṣī vā; pradehastūṣṇaḥ śīto vā bahalo 'bahur aviśoṣī ca;
                        madhyamo 'trālepaḥ | tatra, raktapittaprasādakṛdālepaḥ; pradeho
                        vātaśleṣmapraśamanaḥ śodhano ropaṇaḥ śophavedanāpahaśca;
                        tasyopayogaḥ kṣatākṣateṣu; yas tu kṣateṣūpayujyate sa bhūyaḥ
                        ‘kalka’ iti sañjñāṃ labhate niruddhālepanasañjñaḥ;
                        tenāsrāvasannirodho mṛdutā pūtimāṃsāpakarṣaṇaman antardoṣatā
                        vraṇaśuddhiś ca bhavati ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.7"><l> avidagdheṣu śopheṣu hitamālepanaṃ bhavet |
                        <caesura/>
                         yathāsvaṃ doṣaśamanaṃ dāhakaṇḍūrujāpaham ||7||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.8"><l> tvakprasādanam evāgryaṃ māṃsaraktaprasādanam |
                        dāhapraśamanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rujākaṇḍūvināśanam ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.9"><l> marmadeśeṣu ye rogā guhyeṣv api sadā nṛṇāṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃśodhanāya teṣān tu kuryād ālepanaṃ bhiṣak || 9 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.10"><l> (ṣaḍbhāgaṃ paittike snehaṃ caturbhāgaṃ tu
                        vātike ||
                        <caesura/>
                         aṣṭabhāgaṃ tu kaphaje snehamātrāṃ pradāpayet || 10 ||) </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.11"><l> tasya pramāṇaṃ mahiṣārdracarmotsedham
                        upadiśanti ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.12"><l> na cālepaṃ rātrau prayuñjīta , mā bhūcchaity
                        avihatoṣmaṇastad anirgamād vikārapravṛttir iti ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.13"><l> pradehasādhye vyādhau tu hitam ālepanaṃ divā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pittaraktābhighātotthe saviṣe ca viśeṣataḥ ||13||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.14"><l> na ca paryuṣitaṃ lepaṃ kadācid avacārayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         upary upari lepaṃ ca na kadācit pradāpayet ||14||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="170" n="86a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.15"><l> uṣmāṇaṃ vedanāṃ dāhaṃ ghanatvāj janayet sa hi
                        ||
                        <caesura/>
                         na ca tenaiva lepena pradehaṃ dāpayet punaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śuṣkabhāvāt sa nirvīryo yukto 'pi syād apārthakaḥ ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.16"><l> ata ūrdhvaṃ vraṇabandhanadravyāṇy
                        upadekṣyāmaḥ; tad yathā-
                        kṣaumakārpāsāvikadukūlakauśeyapatrorṇacīnapaṭṭacarmāntarvalkalālābūśakalalatāvidalarajjutūlaphalasantānikālauhānīti
                        teṣāṃ vyādhiṃ kālaṃ cāvekṣyopayogaḥ; prakaraṇataś caiṣām ādeśaḥ
                        ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.17"><l> tatra
                            kośadāmasvastikānuvellitamu(pra)tolīmaṇḍalasthagikāyamakakhaṭvācīnavibandha<pb facs="171" n="87a"/>vitānagophaṇāḥ pañcāṅgī ceti caturdaśa
                        bandhaviśeṣāḥ | teṣāṃ nāmabhir evākṛtayaḥ prāyeṇa vyākhyātāḥ
                        ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.18"><l> tatra kośamaṅguṣṭhāṅguliparvasu vidadhyāt,
                        dāma sambādhe 'ṅge, sandhikūrcakabhrūstanāntaratalakarṇeṣu
                        svastikaṃ, anuvellitaṃ śākhāsu, grīvāmeḍhrayoḥ mu(pra)tolīṃ, vṛtte
                        'ṅge maṇḍalam, aṅguṣṭhāṅgulimeḍhrāgreṣu sthagikāṃ, yamalavraṇayor
                        yamakaṃ, hanuśaṅkhagaṇḍeṣu khaṭvām, apāṅgayoś cīnaṃ,
                        pṛṣṭhodaroraḥsu vibandhaṃ, mūrdhani vitānaṃ,
                        cibukanāsauṣṭhāṃsabastiṣu gophaṇāṃ, jatruṇa ūrdhvaṃ pañcāṅgīm iti;
                        yo vā yasmin śarīrapradeśe suniviṣṭo bhavati taṃ tasmin vidadhyāt
                        ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="172" n="88a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.19"><l> yantraṇam ūrdhvam adhas tiryak ca ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.20"><l> tatra ghanāṃ kavalikāṃ dattvā
                        vāmahastaparikṣepam ṛjum anāviddham asaṅkucitaṃ mṛdu paṭṭaṃ
                        niveśya badhnīyāt | na ca vraṇasyopari kuryād granthim ābādhakaraṃ
                        ca ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.21"><l> na ca vikeśikauṣadhe atisnigdhe atirūkṣe
                        viṣame vā kurvīta; yasmādatisnehāt kledo, raukṣyācchedo,
                        durnyāsādvraṇavartmāvagharṣaṇam iti ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.22"><l> tatra vraṇāyatanaviśeṣād bandhaviśeṣas
                        trividho bhavati gāḍhaḥ, samaḥ, śithila iti ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.23"><l> pīḍayann arujo gāḍhaḥ socchvāsaḥ śithilaḥ
                        smṛtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         naiva gāḍho na śithilaḥ samo bandhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.24"><l> tatra sphikkukṣikakṣāvaṅkṣaṇoruśiraḥsu gāḍhaḥ,
                        śākhāvadanakarṇakaṇṭhameḍhramuṣkapṛṣṭhapārśvodaroraḥsu samaḥ,
                        akṣṇoḥ sandhiṣu ca śithila iti ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.25"><l> tatra paittikaṃ gāḍhasthāne samaṃ badhnīyāt,
                        samasthāne śithilaṃ, śithilasthāne naiva; evaṃ śoṇitaduṣṭaṃ ca;
                        ślaiṣmikaṃ śithilasthāne samaṃ, samasthāne gāḍhaṃ, gāḍhasthāne
                        gāḍhataraṃ; evaṃ vātaduṣṭaṃ ca ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.26"><l> tatra paittikaṃ śaradi grīṣme dvirahno
                        badhnīyāt, raktopadrutamapyevaṃ; ślaiṣmikaṃ hemantavasantayo<pb facs="173" n="89a"/>stryahāt , vātopadrutamapyevam |
                        evamabhyūhya bandhaviparyayaṃ ca kuryāt ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.27"><l> tatra, samaśithilasthāneṣu gāḍhaṃ baddhe
                        vikeśikauṣadhanair arthakyaṃ śophavedanāprādurbhāvaś ca,
                        gāḍhasamasthāneṣu śithilaṃ baddhe vikeśikauṣadhapatanaṃ
                        paṭṭasañcārādvraṇavartmāvagharṣaṇamiti; gāḍhaśithilasthāneṣu samaṃ
                        baddhe ca guṇābhāva iti ||27|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.28"><l> aviparītabandhe vedanopaśāntir asṛkprasādo
                        mārdavaṃ ca ||28|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.29"><l> abadhyamāno
                        daṃśamaśakatṛṇakāṣṭhopalapāṃśuśītavātātapaprabhṛtibhir viśeṣair
                        abhihanyate vraṇaḥ, vividhavedanopadrutaś ca duṣṭatām upaiti,
                        ālepanādīni cāsya viśoṣam upayānti ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.30"><l> cūrṇitaṃ mathitaṃ bhagnaṃ viśliṣṭam atipātitam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         asthisnāyusirācchinnam āśu bandhena rohati ||30|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.31"><l> sukhamevaṃ vraṇī śete sukhaṃ gacchati tiṣṭhati
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         sukhaṃ śayyāsanasthasya kṣipraṃ saṃrohati vraṇaḥ ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.32"><l> abandhyāḥ pittaraktābhighātaviṣanimittā yadā
                        śophadāhapākarāgatodavedanābhibhūtāḥ kṣārāgnidagdhāḥ pākāt
                        prakuthitapraśīrṇamāṃsāś ca bhavanti ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.33"><l> kuṣṭhinām agnidagdhānāṃ piḍakā madhumehinām |
                        <caesura/>
                         karṇikāś conduruviṣe viṣajuṣṭāś ca ye vraṇāḥ ||33|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.34"><l> māṃsapāke na badhyante gudapāke ca dāruṇe |
                        <caesura/>
                         svabuddhyā cāpi vibhajet kṛtyākṛtyāṃś ca buddhimān ||34|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.35"><l> deśaṃ doṣaṃ ca vijñāya vraṇaṃ ca vraṇakovidaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         ṛtūṃś ca parisaṅkhyāya tato bandhān niveśayet ||35||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.36"><l> ūrdhvaṃ tiryag adhastāc ca yantraṇā trividhā
                        smṛtā |
                        <caesura/>
                         yathā ca badhyate bandhas tathā vakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||36|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.37"><l> ghanāṃ kavalikāṃ dattvā mṛdu caivāpi paṭṭakam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vikeśikāmauṣadhaṃ ca nātisnigdhaṃ samācaret ||37|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.38"><l> prakledayatyatisnigdhā tathā rūkṣā kṣiṇoti ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         yuktasnehā ropayati durnyastā vartma gharṣati ||38|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.39"><l> viṣamaṃ ca vraṇaṃ kuryāt stambhayet
                        srāvayettathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         yathāvraṇaṃ viditvā tu yogaṃ vaidyaḥ prayojayet ||39|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.40"><l> pittaje raktaje vā'pi sakṛd eva parikṣipet |
                        <caesura/>
                         asakṛt kaphaje vā'pi vātaje ca vicakṣaṇaḥ ||40|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.41"><l> talena pratipīḍyātha srāvayed anulomataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvāṃś ca bandhān gūḍhāntān sandhīṃś ca viniveśayet ||41|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.42"><l> oṣṭhasyāpyeṣa sandhāne yathoddiṣṭo vidhiḥ
                        smṛtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         buddhyotprekṣyābhiyuktena tathā cāsthiṣu jānatā ||42|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.43"><l> uttiṣṭhato niṣaṇṇasya śayanaṃ cādhigacchataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         gacchato vividhair yānair nāsya duṣyati sa vraṇaḥ ||43|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="174" n="90a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.44"><l> ye ca syur māṃsasaṃsthā vai tvaggatāś ca tathā
                        vraṇāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sandhyasthikoṣṭhalaprāptāś ca sirāsnāyugatās tathā ||44|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.45"><l> tathā'vagāḍhagambhīrāḥ sarvato viṣamasthitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         naite sādhayituṃ śakyā ṛte bandhād bhavanti hi ||45|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.18.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        vraṇālepanabandhavidhirnāmāṣṭādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||18|| </l></lg>

                        navadhānyamāṣatilakalāyakulatthaniṣpāvaharitakaśākāmlalavaṇakaṭukaguḍapiṣṭavikṛtivallūraśuṣkaśākājāvikānūpaudaka-

                        <caesura/>
                         tataḥ śopharujāsrāvadāhapākānavāpnuyāt ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.19.23"><l> sadā nīcanakharomṇā śucinā śuklavāsasā
