                <div n="26" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.1"><l> athātaḥ pranaṣṭaśalyavijñānīyam adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantiriḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.3"><l> ‘śala’ ‘śvala’ āśugamane dhātūḥ; tayor ādyasya
                        śalyam iti rūpam ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.4"><l> tad dvividhaṃ śārīram āgantukaṃ ca ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.5"><l> sarvaśarīrābādhakaraṃ śalyaṃ, tadihopadiśyata
                        ity ataḥ śalyaśāstram ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.6"><l> tatra śārīraṃ dantaromanakhādi dhātavo 'nnamalā
                        doṣāś ca duṣṭāḥ; āgantv api śārīraśalyavyatirekeṇa yāvanto bhāvā
                        duḥkham utpādayanti ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.7"><l> adhikāro hi lohaveṇuvṛkṣatṛṇaśṛṅgāsthimayeṣu;
                        tatrāpi viśeṣato loheṣveva , viśasanārthopapanna<pb facs="206" n="122a"/>tvāllohasya; lohānām api durvāratvād aṇumukhatvād
                        dūraprayojanakaratvāc ca śara evādhikṛtaḥ | sa ca dvividhaḥ karṇī
                        , ślakṣṇaśca; prāyeṇa vividhavṛkṣapatrapuṣpaphalatulyākṛtayo
                        vyākhyātāḥ, vyālamṛgapakṣivakrasadṛśāś ca ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.8"><l> sarvaśalyānāṃ tu mahatāmaṇūnāṃ vā pañcavidho
                        gativiśeṣa ūrdhvam adho 'rvācīnas tiryag ṛjur iti ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.9"><l> tāni vegakṣayāt pratighātād vā tvagādiṣu
                        vraṇavastuṣvatiṣṭhante, dhamanīsroto 'sthivivarapeśīprabhṛtiṣu vā
                        śarīrapradeśeṣu ||9||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.10"><l> tatra śalyalakṣaṇam ucyamānam upadhāraya | tad
                        dvividhaṃ -- sāmānyaṃ, vaiśeṣikaṃ ca | śyāvaṃ piḍakācitaṃ
                        śophavedanāvantaṃ muhur muhuḥ śoṇitāsrāviṇaṃ budbudavad unnataṃ
                        mṛdumāṃsaṃ ca vraṇaṃ jānīyāt saśalyo 'yam iti | sāmānyam etal
                        lakṣaṇam uktam | vaiśeṣikaṃ tu -- tvaggate vivarṇaḥ śopho bhavaty
                        āyataḥ kaṭhinaś ca; māṃsagate śophābhi(ti)vṛddhiḥ
                        śalyamārgānupasaṃrohaḥ pīḍanāsahiṣṇutā coṣapākau ca;
                        peśyantarasthe 'py etad eva coṣaśophavarjaṃ; sirāgate sirādhmānam
                        sirāśūlaṃ sirāśophaś ca; snāyugate snāyujālotkṣepaṇaṃ saṃrambhaś
                        cogrā ruk ca; srotogate srotasāṃ svakarmaguṇahāniḥ; dhamanīsthe
                        saphenaṃ raktam īrayann anilaḥ saśabdo nirgacchaty aṅgamardaḥ
                        pipāsā hṛllāsaś ca; asthigate vividhavedanāprādurbhāvaḥ śophaś ca
                        asthivivaragate 'sthipūrṇatā'sthitodaḥ saṃharṣo balavāṃś ca;
                        sandhigate 'sthivacceṣṭoparamaś ca; koṣṭhagata āṭopānāhau
                        mūtrapurīṣāhāradarśanaṃ ca vraṇamukhāt; marmagate marmaviddhāvac
                        ceṣṭate | sūkṣmagatiṣu śalyeṣv etāny eva lakṣaṇāny aspaṣṭāni
                        bhavanti || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.11"><l><pb facs="207" n="123a"/> mahānty alpāni vā
                        śuddhadehānām anulomasanniviṣṭāni rohanti viśeṣataḥ
                        kaṇṭhasrotaḥsirātvakpeśyasthivivareṣaḥ |
                        doṣaprakopavyāyāmābhighātājīrṇebhyaḥ pracalitāni punar bādhante
                        ||11|| </l></lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.12"><l> tatra, tvakpranaṣṭe snigdhasvinnāyāṃ
                        mṛnmāṣayavagodhūmagomayamṛditāyāṃ tvaci yatra saṃrambho vedanā vā
                        bhavati tatra śalyaṃ vijānīyāt , styānaghṛtamṛccandanakalkair vā
                        pradigdhāyāṃ śalyoṣmaṇā''śu visarati ghṛtam upaśuṣyati cālepo
                        yatra tatra śalyaṃ vijānīyāt ; māṃsapranaṣṭe snehasvedādibhiḥ
                        kriyāviśeṣair aviruddhair āturam upapādayet, karśitasya tu
                        śithilībhūtam anavabaddhaṃ kṣubhyamāṇaṃ yatra saṃrambhaṃ vedanāṃ
                        vā janayati tatra śalyaṃ vijānīyāt ; koṣṭhāsthisandhipeśīvivareṣv
                        avasthitam evam eva parīkṣeta; sirādhamanīsrotaḥsnāyupranaṣṭe
                        khaṇḍacakrasaṃyukte yāne vyādhitamāropyāśu viṣame 'dhvani yāyāt
                        yatra saṃrambho vedanā vā bhavati tatra śalyaṃ vijānīyāt ;
                        asthipranaṣṭe snehasvedopapannānyasthīni bandhanapīḍanābhyāṃ
                        bhṛśam upācaret, yatra saṃrambho vedanā bhavati tatra śalyaṃ
                        vijānīyāt ; sandhipranaṣṭe snehasvedopapannān sandhīn
                        prasaraṇākuñcanabandhanapīḍanaiḥ bhṛśam upācaret, yatra saṃrambho
                        vedanā vā bhavati tatra śalyaṃ vijānīyāt , sandhipranaṣṭe
                        snehasvedopapannān sandhīn prasaraṇākuñcanabandhanapīḍanairbhṛśam
                        upācaret; yatra saṃrambho vedanā vā bhavati tatra śalyaṃ vijānīyāt
                        , marmapranaṣṭe tv ananyabhāvān marmaṇām uktaṃ parīkṣaṇaṃ bhavati
                        ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.13"><l> sāmānyalakṣam api ca
                        hastiskandhāśvapṛṣṭhaparvatadrumārohaṇadhanurvyāyāmadrutayānaniyuddhādhvagamanalaṅghanaplavanaprataraṇa-
                        vyāyāmairjṛmbhodgārakāsakṣavathuṣṭhīvanahasanaprāṇāyāmair
                        vātamūtrapurīṣaśukrotsargair vā yatra saṃrambho vedanā vā bhavati
                        tatra śalyaṃ vijānīyāt ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.14"><l><pb facs="208" n="124a"/> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         yasmiṃs todādayo deśe suptatā gurutā'pi ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         ghaṭṭate bahuśo yatra śūyate rujyate 'pi ca ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.15"><l> āturaś cāpi yaṃ deśam abhīkṣṇaṃ parirakṣati |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃvāhyamāno bahuśas tatra śalyaṃ vinirdiśet ||15||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.16"><l> alpābādham aśūnaṃ ca nīrujaṃ nirupadravam |
                        <caesura/>
                         prasannaṃ mṛduparyantaṃ nirāghaṭṭamanunnatam ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.17"><l> eṣaṇyā sarvato dṛṣṭvā yathāmārgaṃ cikitsakaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prasārākuñcanān nūnaṃ niḥśalyam iti nirdiśet ||17||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.18"><l> asthyātmakaṃ bhajyate tu śalyamantaś ca
                        śīryate |
                        <caesura/>
                         prāyo nirbhujyate śārṅgam āyasaṃ ceti niścayaḥ ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.19"><l> vārkṣavaiṇavatārṇāni nirhriyante tu no yadi |
                        <caesura/>
                         pacanti raktaṃ māṃsaṃ ca kṣipram etāni dehinām ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.20"><l> kānakaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ raitikaṃ trapu sīsakam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         cirasthānādvilīyante pittatejaḥpratāpanāt ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.21"><l> svabhāvaśītā mṛdavo ye cānye 'pīdṛśā matāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dravībhūtāḥ śarīre 'sminn ekatvaṃ yānti dhātubhiḥ ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.22"><l> viṣāṇadantakeśāsthiveṇudārupalāni tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         śalyāni na viśīryante śarīre mṛnmayāni ca ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.23"><l> dvividhaṃ pañcagatimattvagādivraṇavastuṣu |
                        <caesura/>
                         yo vetti viṣṭitaṃ śalyaṃ sa rājñaḥ kartumarhati ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        pranaṣṭaśalyavijñānīyo nāma ṣaḍviṃśatitamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||26|| </l></lg>





                </div>


                <div n="27" type="adhyāya">

                    <pb facs="209" n="125a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.1"><l> athātaḥ śalyāpanayanīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || 1 || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || 2 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.3"><l> śalyaṃ dvividham avabaddham anavabaddhaṃ ca ||
                        3 || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.4"><l> tatra samāsenānavabaddhaśalyoddharaṇārthaṃ
                        pañcadaśa hetūn vakṣyāmaḥ | tad yathā- svabhāvaḥ, pācanaṃ,
                        bhedanaṃ, dāraṇaṃ, pīḍanaṃ, pramārjanaṃ, nirdhmāpanaṃ, vamanaṃ,
                        virecanaṃ, prakṣālanaṃ, pratimarśaḥ, pravāhaṇam, ācūṣaṇam,
                        ayaskānto, harṣaś ceti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.5"><l> tatrāśrukṣavathūdgārakāsamūtrapurīṣānilaiḥ
                        svabhāvabalapravṛttair nayanādibhyaḥ patati | māṃsāvagāḍhaṃ
                        śalyamavidahyamānaṃ pācayitvā prakothāttasya pūyaśoṇitavegād
                        gauravādvā patati | pakvamabhidyamānaṃ bhedayed dārayed vā |
                        bhinnamanirasyamānaṃ pīḍanīyaiḥ pīḍayet pāṇibhir vā |
                        aṇūnyakṣaśalyāni pariṣecanādhmāpanair bālavastrapāṇibhiḥ
                        pramārjayet | āhāraśeṣaśleṣmahīnāṇuśalyāni
                        śvasanotkāsanapradhamanair nirdhamet | annaśalyāni
                        vamanāṅgulipratimarśaprabhṛtibhiḥ | virecanaiḥ pakvāśayagatāni |
                        vraṇadoṣāśayagatāni prakṣālanaiḥ | vātamūtrapurīṣagarbhasaṅgeṣu
                        pravāhaṇam uktam | mārutodakasaviṣarudhiraduṣṭastanyeṣv ācūṣaṇam
                        āsyena viṣāṇair vā | anulomam anavabaddham akarṇam
                        analpavraṇamukham ayaskāntena | hṛdy avasthitam anekakāraṇotpannaṃ
                        śokaśalyaṃ harṣeṇeti ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="210" n="126a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.6"><l> sarvaśalyānāṃ tu mahatām aṇūnāṃ vā dvāv
                        evāharaṇahetū bhavataḥ- pratilomo 'nulomaś ca ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.7"><l> tatra pratilomamarvācīnamānayet, anulomaṃ
                        parācīnam ||7||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="210" n="126b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.8"><l>uttuṇditaṃ chitvā nirghātayec chedanīyamukham ||
                        8 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.9"><l> chedanīyamukhāny api
                        kukṣivakṣaḥkakṣāvaṅkṣaṇaparśukāntarapatitāni ca hastaśakyaṃ
                        yathāmārgeṇa hastenaivāpahartuṃ prayateta ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.10"><l> hastenaivāpahartum aśakyaṃ viśasya śastreṇa
                        yantreṇāpaharet ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="211" n="127a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.11"><l> bhavati cātra -
                        <caesura/>
                         śītalena jalenainaṃ mūrcchantamav asecayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃrakṣedasya marmāṇi muhur āśvāsayec ca tam ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.12"><l> tataḥ śalyam uddhṛtya nirlohitaṃ vraṇaṃ kṛtvā
                        svedārham agnighṛtaprabhṛtibhiḥ saṃsvedyāvadahya pradihya
                        sarpirmadhubhyāṃ baddhvā''cārikam upadiśet | (sirāsnāyuvilagnaṃ
                        śalākādibhir vimocyāpanayet; śvayathugrastavāraṅgaṃ samavapīḍya
                        śvayathuṃ; durbalavāraṅgaṃ kuśādibhir baddhvā|) ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.13"><l> hṛdayamabhito vartamānaṃ śalyaṃ śītajalādibhir
                        udvejitasyāpahared yathāmārgaṃ; durupaharam anyato 'pabādhyamānaṃ
                        pāṭayitvoddharet ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.14-16"><l> asthivivarapraviṣṭam asthividaṣṭaṃ
                        vā'vagṛhya pādābhyāṃ yantreṇāpaharet, aśakyamevaṃ vā balavadbhiḥ
                        suparigṛhītasya yantreṇa grāhayitvā śalyavāraṅgaṃ pravibhujya
                        dhanurguṇair baddhvaikataś cāsya pañcāṅgyām upasaṃyatasyāśvasya
                        vaktrakavike badhnīyāt, athainaṃ kaśayā tāḍayed yathonnāmayañ śiro
                        vegena śalyam uddharati; dṛḍhāṃ vā vṛkṣaśākhām avanamya tasyāṃ
                        pūrvavad baddhvoddharet ||14||
                        <caesura/>
                         adeśottuṇḍitamaṣṭhīlāśmamudgarāṇāmanyatamasya prahāreṇa vicālya
                        yathāmārgam eva yantreṇa ||15||
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="212" n="128a"/>vimṛditakarṇāni
                        karṇavantyanābādhakaradeśottuṇḍitāni purastād eva ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.17"><l> jātuṣe kaṇṭhāsakte kaṇṭhe nāḍīṃ
                        praveśyāgnitaptāṃ ca śalākāṃ, tayā'vagṛhya śītābhir adbhiḥ
                        pariṣicya sthirībhūtaṃ śalyam uddharet ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.18-19"><l> ajātuṣaṃ tu jatumadhūcchiṣṭapraliptayā
                        śalākayā pūrvakalpenety eke ||18|| asthiśalyam anyad vā
                        tiryakkaṇṭhāsaktam avekṣya keśoṇḍukaṃ dṛḍhaikasūtrabaddhaṃ
                        dravabhaktopahitaṃ pāyayed ā kaṇṭhāt pūrṇakoṣṭhaṃ ca vāmayet,
                        vamataś ca śalyaikadeśasaktaṃ jñātvā sūtraṃ sahasā tv ākṣipet;
                        mṛdunā vā dantadhāvanakūrcakenāpaharet praṇuded vā 'antaḥ |
                        kṣatakaṇṭhāya ca madhusarpiṣī leḍhuṃ prayacchet triphalācūrṇaṃ vā
                        madhuśarkarāvimiśram || 19 ||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="212" n="128b"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.20"><l> udakapūrṇodaram avākśirasam avapīḍayed
                        dhunīyād vāmayed vā bhasmarāśau vā nikhaned ā mukhāt || 20 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.21"><l> grāsaśalye tu kaṇṭhāsakte niḥśaṅkam
                        anavabuddhaṃ skandhe muṣṭinā 'bhihanyāt, snehaṃ madyaṃ pānīyaṃ vā
                        pāyayet || 21 ||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.22"><l> bāhurajjulatāpāśaiḥ kaṇṭhapīḍanād vāyuḥ
                        prakupitaḥ śleṣmāṇaṃ kopayitvā sroto niruṇaddhi, lālāsrāvaṃ
                        phenāgamanaṃ sañjñānāśaṃ cāpādayati; tam abhyajya saṃsvedya
                        śirovirecanaṃ tasmai tīkṣṇaṃ vidadhyād rasaṃ ca vātaghnaṃ dadyād
                        iti ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.23"><l> bhavanti cātra -
                        <caesura/>
                         śalyākṛtiviśeṣāṃś ca sthānāny āvekṣya buddhimān |
                        <caesura/>
                         tathā yantrapṛthaktvaṃ ca samyak śalyam athāharet ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.24"><l> karṇavanti tu śalyāni duḥkhāhāryāṇi yāni ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         ādadīta bhiṣak tasmāt tāni yuktyā samāhitaḥ ||24||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.25"><l> etair upāyaiḥ śalyaṃ tu naiva niryātyate yadi
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         matyā nipuṇayā vaidyo yantrayogaiś ca nirharet ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.26"><l> śothapākau rujaś cogrāḥ kuryāc chalyamanāhṛtam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaikalyaṃ maraṇaṃ cāpi tasmād yatnādvinirharet ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.27.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        śalyāpanayanīyo nāma saptaviṃśatitamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||27|| </l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="28" type="adhyāya">

                    <pb facs="213" n="129a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.1"><l> athāto viparītāviparītavraṇavijñānīyam adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.3"><l> phalāgnijalavṛṣṭīnāṃ puṣpadhūmāmbudā yathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         khyāpayanti bhaviṣyatvaṃ tathā riṣṭāni pañcatām || </l></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.4"><l> tāni saukṣmyāt pramādād vā tathaivāśu
                        vyatikramāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         gṛhyante nodgatāny ajñair mumūrṣor na tv asambhavāt ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.5"><l> dhruvaṃ tu maraṇaṃ riṣṭe brāhmaṇais tat
                        kilāmalaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasāyanatapojapyatatparair vā nivāryate ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.6"><l> nakṣatrapīḍā bahudhā yathā kālaṃ vipacyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         tathaivāriṣṭapākaṃ ca bruvate bahavo janāḥ ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="214" n="130a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.7"><l> asiddhim āpnuyāl loke pratikurvan gatāyuṣaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ato 'riṣṭāni yatnena lakṣayet kuśalo bhiṣak ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.8"><l> gandhavarṇarasādīnāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ svabhāvataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaikṛtaṃ yat tad ācaṣṭe vraṇinaḥ pakvalakṣaṇam ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.9"><l> kaṭus tīkṣṇaś ca visraś ca gandhas tu
                        pavanādibhiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         lohagandhis tu raktena vyāmiśraḥ sānnipātikaḥ ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.10"><l> lājātasītailasamāḥ kiñcidvisrāś ca gandhataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         jñeyāḥ prakṛtigandhāḥ syur ato 'nyadgandhavaikṛtam ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.11"><l> madyāgurvājyasumanāpadmacandanacampakaiḥ |
                        sagandhā divyagandhāś ca mumūrṣūṇāṃ vraṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||11||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.12"><l> śvavājimūṣikadhvāṅkṣapūtivallūramatkuṇaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sagandhāḥ paṅkagandhāś ca bhūmigandhāś ca garhitāḥ ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.13"><l> kuṅkumadhyāmakaṅkuṣṭhasavarṇāḥ pittakopataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         na dahyante na cūṣyante bhiṣak tān parivarjayet ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.14"><l> aṇḍūmantaḥ sthirāḥ śvetāḥ snigdhāḥ
                        kaphanimittataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dūyante vā'pi dahyante bhiṣak tān parivarjayet ||14||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.15"><l> kṛṣṇās tu ye tanusrāvā vātajā marmatāpinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         svalpām api na kurvanti rujaṃ tān parivarjayet ||15||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.16"><l> kṣveḍanti ghurghurāyante jvalantīva ca ye
                        vraṇāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tvaṅmāṃsasthāś ca pavanaṃ saśabdaṃ visṛjanti ye ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.17"><l> ye ca marmasvasambhūtā bhavanty
                        atyarthavedanāḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         dahyante cāntaratyarthaṃ bahiḥ śītāś ca ye vraṇāḥ ||17||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.18"><l> dahyante bahir atyarthaṃ bhavantyantaśca
                        śītalāḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         śaktidhvajarathāḥ kuntavājivāraṇagovṛṣāḥ ||18|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.19"><l> yeṣu cāpy avabhāseran prāsādākṛtayas tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         cūrṇāvakīrṇā iva ye bhānticānavacūrṇitāḥ ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="215" n="131a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.20"><l> prāṇamāṃsakṣayaśvāsakāsārocakapīḍitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pravṛddhapūyarudhirā vraṇā yeṣāṃ ca marmasu ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.21"><l> kriyābhiḥ samyag ārabdhā na siddhyanti ca ye
                        vraṇāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         varjayet tān api prājñaḥ saṃrakṣannātmano yaśaḥ ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.28.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        viparītāviparītavraṇavijñānīyo nāmāṣṭāviṃśatitamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||28||
                    </l></lg>
                </div>


                <div n="29" type="adhyāya">


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.1"><l> athāto viparītāviparītasvapnanidarśanīyam
                        adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.3"><l> dūtadarśanasambhāṣā veṣāś ceṣṭitam eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         ṛkṣaṃ velā tithiś caiva nimittaṃ śakuno 'nilaḥ ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.4"><l> deśo vaidyasya vāgdehamanasāṃ ca viceṣṭitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kathayanty āturagataṃ śubhaṃ vā yadi vā'śubham ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.5"><l> pākha(ṣa)ṇḍāśramavarṇānāṃ sapakṣāḥ
                        karmasiddhaye |
                        <caesura/>
                         ta eva viparītāḥ syur dūtāḥ karmavipattaye ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.6"><l> napuṃsakaṃ strī bahavo naikakāryā asūyakāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         gardabhoṣṭrarathaprāptāḥ prāptā vā syuḥ paramparāḥ ||6||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.7"><l> vaidyaṃ ya upasarpanti dūtāste cāpi garhitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāśadaṇḍāyudhadharāḥ pāṇḍuretaravāsasaḥ ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.8"><l> ārdrajīrṇāpasavyaikamalinoddhvastavāsasaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nyūnādhikāṅgā udvignā vikṛtā raudrarūpiṇaḥ ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.9"><l> rūkṣaniṣṭhuravaktārastv amaṅgalyābhidhāyinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         chindantast ṛṇakāṣṭhāni spṛśanto nāsikāṃ stanam ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.10"><l> vastrāntānāmikākeśanakharomadaśāspṛśaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         srotovarodhahṛdgaṇḍamūrdhoraḥkukṣipāṇayaḥ ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.11"><l> kapālopalabhasmāsthituṣāṅgārakarāś ca ye |
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="216" n="132a"/> vilikhanto mahīṃ kiñcin muñcanto
                        loṣṭabhedinaḥ ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.12"><l> tailakardamadigdhāṅgā raktasraganulepanāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         phalaṃ pakvam asāraṃ vā gṛhītvā'nyac ca tadvidham ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.13"><l> nakhair nakhāntaraṃ vā'pi kareṇa caraṇaṃ tathā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         upānaccarmahastā vā vikṛtavyādhipīḍitāḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.14"><l> vāmācārā rudantaś ca śvāsino vikṛtekṣaṇāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yāmyāṃ diśi prāñjalayo viṣamaikapade sthitāḥ ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.15abcd"><l> vaidyaṃ ya upasarpanti dūtās te cāpi
                        garhitāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dakṣiṇābhimukhaṃ deśe tvaśucau vā hutāśanam | </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.15ef"><l> jvalayantaṃ pacantaṃ vā krūrakarmaṇi
                        codyatam ||15|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.16"><l> nagnaṃ bhūmau śayānaṃ vā vegotsargeṣu vā'śucim
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakīrṇakeśam abhyaktaṃ svinnaṃ viklavam eva vā ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.17"><l> vaidyaṃ ya upasarpanti dūtās te cāpi garhitāḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyasya paitrye daive vā kārye cotpātadarśane ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.18"><l> madhyāhne cārdharātre vā sandhyayoḥ kṛttikāsu
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         ārdrāśleṣāmaghāmūlapūrvāsu bharaṇīṣu ca ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.19"><l> caturthyāṃ vā navamyāṃ vā ṣaṣṭhyāṃ
                        sandhidineṣu ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyaṃ ya upasarpanti dūtās te cāpi garhitāḥ ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.20"><l> svinnābhitaptā madhyāhne jvalanasya samīpataḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         garhitāḥ pittarogeṣu dūtā vaidyam upāgatāḥ ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.21"><l> ta eva kapharogeṣu karmasiddhikarāḥ smṛtāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         etena śeṣaṃ vyākhyātaṃ buddhvā saṃvibhajet tu tat ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.22"><l> raktapittātisāreṣu prameheṣu tathaiva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         praśasto jalarodheṣu dūtavaidyasamāgamaḥ ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.23"><l> vijñāyaivaṃ vibhāgaṃ tu śeṣaṃ budhyeta
                        paṇḍitaḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         śuklavāsāḥ śucir gauraḥ śyāmo vā priyadarśanaḥ ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.24"><l> svasyāṃ jātau svagotro vā dūtaḥ kāryakaraḥ
                        smṛtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         goyānenāgatas tuṣṭaḥ pādābhyāṃ śubhaceṣṭitaḥ ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="217" n="133a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.25"><l> smṛtimān vidhikālajñaḥ svatantraḥ
                        pratipattimān |
                        <caesura/>
                         alaṅkṛto maṅgalavān dūtaḥ kāryakaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||25||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.26"><l> svasthaṃ prāṅmukham āsīnaṃ same deśe śucau
                        śucim |
                        <caesura/>
                         upasarpati yo vaidyaṃ sa ca kāryakaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.27"><l> māṃsodakumbhātapatravipravāraṇagovṛṣāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śuklavarṇāś ca pūjyante prasthāne darśanaṃ gatāḥ ||27|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.28"><l> strī putriṇī savatsā gaur vardhamānam alaṅkṛtā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kanyā matsyāḥ phalaṃ cāmaṃ svastikaṃ modakā dadhi ||28|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.29"><l> hiraṇyākṣatapātraṃ vā ratnāni sumano nṛpaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         apraśānto 'nalo vājī haṃsaś cāpaḥ śikhī tathā ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.30"><l> brahmadundubhijīmūtaśaṅkhaveṇurathasvanāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         siṃhagovṛṣanādāś ca hre(he)ṣitaṃ gajabṛṃhitam ||30|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.31"><l> śastaṃ haṃsarutaṃ nṝṇāṃ kauśikaṃ caiva
                        vāmataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prasthāne yāyinaḥ śreṣṭhā vācaś ca hṛdayaṅgamāḥ ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.32"><l> patrapuṣpaphalopetān sakṣīrānnīrujo drumān |
                        <caesura/>
                         āśritā vā nabhoveśmadhvajatoraṇavedikāḥ ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.33"><l> dikṣu śāntāsu vaktāro madhuraṃ pṛṣṭhato 'nugāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāmā vā dakṣiṇā vā'pi śakunāḥ karmasiddhaye ||33|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.34"><l> śuṣke 'śanihate 'patre vallīnaddhe sakaṇṭake |
                        <caesura/>
                         vṛkṣe 'thavā'śmabhasmāsthiviṭtuṣāṅgārapāṃśuṣu ||34|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.35"><l> caity avalmīkaviṣamasthitā dīptakharasvarāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         purato dikṣu dīptāsu vaktāro nārthasādhakāḥ ||35|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.36"><l> punnāmānaḥ khagā vāmāḥ strīsañjñā dakṣiṇāḥ
                        śubhāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dakṣiṇād vāmagamanaṃ praśastaṃ śvaśṛgālayoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vāmaṃ nakulacāṣāṇāṃ nobhayaṃ śaśasarpayoḥ ||36|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.37ab"><l> bhāsakauśikayoś caiva na praśastaṃ
                        kilobhayam | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.37cd"><l> darśanaṃ vā rutaṃ vā'pi na godhākṛkalāsayoḥ
                        ||37|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.38"><l> dūtair aniṣṭais tulyānām aśastaṃ darśanaṃ
                        nṛṇām |
                        <caesura/>
                         kulatthatilakārpāsatuṣapāṣāṇabhasmanām ||38|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.39"><l> pātraṃ neṣṭaṃ tathā'ṅgāratailakardamapūritam |
                        <caesura/>
                         prasannetaramadyānāṃ pūrṇaṃ vā raktasarṣapaiḥ ||39|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.40"><l> śavakāṣṭhapalāśānāṃ śuṣkāṇāṃ pathi saṅgamāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         neṣyante patitāntasthadīnāndharipavas tathā ||40|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="218" n="134a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.41"><l> mṛduḥ śīto 'nukūlaś ca sugandhiś cānilaḥ
                        śubhaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kharoṣṇo 'niṣṭagandhaś ca pratilomaś ca garhitaḥ ||41|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.42"><l> granthyarbudādiṣu sadā chedaśabdas tu pūjitaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vidradhyudaragulmeṣu bhedaśabdas tathaiva ca ||42|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.43"><l> raktapittātisāreṣu ruddhaśabdaḥ praśasyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         evaṃ vyādhiviśeṣeṇa nimittam upadhārayet ||43|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.44"><l> tathaivākruṣṭahākaṣṭamākrandaruditasvanāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         chardyāṃ vātapurīṣāṇāṃ śabdo vai gardabhoṣṭrayoḥ ||44|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.45"><l> pratiṣiddhaṃ tathā bhagnaṃ kṣutaṃ skhalitam
                        āhatam |
                        <caesura/>
                         daurmanasyaṃ ca vaidyasya yātrāyāṃ na praśasyate ||45|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.46"><l> praveśe 'py etad uddeśād avekṣyaṃ ca
                        tathā''ture |
                        <caesura/>
                         pratidvāraṃ gṛhe cāsya punaretanna gaṇyate ||46|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.47"><l> keśabhasmāsthikāṣṭhāśmatuṣakārpāsakaṇṭakāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         khaṭvordhvapādā madyāpo vasā tailaṃ tilāstṛṇam ||47|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.48"><l> napuṃsakavyaṅgabhagnanagnamuṇḍāsitāmbarāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prasthāne vā praveśe vā neṣyante darśanaṃ gatāḥ ||48|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.49"><l> bhāṇḍānāṃ saṅkarasthānāṃ sthānāt sañcāraṇaṃ
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         nikhātotpāṭanaṃ bhaṅgaḥ patanaṃ nirgamas tathā ||49|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.50"><l> vaidyāsanāvasādo vā rogī vā syādadhomukhaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyaṃ sambhāṣamāṇo 'ṅgaṃ kuḍyamā staraṇāni vā ||50||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="219" n="135a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.51"><l> pramṛjyādvā dhunīyād vā karau pṛṣṭhaṃ śiras
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         hastaṃ cākṛṣya vaidyasya nyasec chirasi corasi ||51|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.52ab"><l> yo vaidyam unmukhaḥ paśyann unmārṣṭi svāṅgam
                        āturaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.52cd"><l> na sa sidhyati vaidyo vā gṛhe yasya na
                        pūjyate ||52|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.53"><l> bhavane pūjyate vā'pi yasya vaidyaḥ sa
                        sidhyati
                        <caesura/>
                         śubhaṃ śubheṣu dūtādiṣv aśubhaṃ hy aśubheṣu ca ||53|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.54"><l> āturasya dhruvaṃ tasmād dūtādīn lakṣayed
                        bhiṣak
                        <caesura/>
                         svapnān ataḥ pravakṣyāmi maraṇāya śubhāya ca ||54|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.55"><l> suhṛdo yāṃś ca paśyanti vyādhito vā svayaṃ
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         snehābhyaktaśarīras tu karabhavyālagardabhaiḥ ||55||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.56"><l> varāhair mahiṣair vā'pi yo yāyād dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         raktāmbaradharā kṛṣṇā hasantī muktamūrdhajā ||56|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.57"><l> yaṃ cākarṣati baddhvā strī nṛtyantī
                        dakṣiṇāmukham |
                        <caesura/>
                         antāvasāyibhir yo vā 'kṛṣyate dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||57||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.58"><l> pariṣvajeran yaṃ vā'pi pretāḥ pravrajitā(na)s
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         muhur āghrāyate yas tu śvāpadair vikṛtānanaiḥ ||58||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.59"><l> piben madhu ca tailaṃ ca yo vā paṅke 'vasīdati
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         paṅkapradigdhagātro vā pranṛtyet prahasettathā ||59||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.60"><l> nirambaraś ca yo raktāṃ dhārayec chirasā
                        srajam |
                        <caesura/>
                         yasya vaṃśo nalo vā'pi tālo vorasi jāyate ||60||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.61"><l> yaṃ vā matsyo grased yo vā jananīṃ praviśen
                        naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         parvatāgrāt pated yo vā śvabhre vā tamasā''vṛte ||61||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.62"><l> hriyeta srotasā yo vā yo vā mauṇḍyamav āpnuyāt
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         parājīyeta badhyeta kākādyair vā'bhibhūyate ||62|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.63"><l> patanaṃ tārakādīnāṃ praṇāśaṃ dīpacakṣuṣoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yaḥ paśyed devatānāṃ vā prakampam avanes tathā ||63||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.64"><l> yasya chardir vireko vā daśanāḥ prapatanti vā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śālmalīṃ kiṃśukaṃ yūpaṃ valmīkaṃ pāribhadrakam ||64|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.65"><l> puṣpāḍhyaṃ kovidāraṃ vā citāṃ vā yo 'dhirohati
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kārpāsatailapiṇyākalohāni lavaṇaṃ tilān ||65||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.66ab"><l> labhetāśnīta vā pakvam annaṃ yaś ca pibet
                        surām | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.66cd"><l> svasthaḥ sa labhate vyādhiṃ vyādhito
                        mṛtyumṛcchati ||66||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.67"><l> yathāsvaṃ prakṛtisvapno vismṛto vihatas tathā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         cintākṛto divā dṛṣṭo bhavanty aphaladās tu te ||67|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="220" n="136a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.68"><l> jvaritānāṃ śunā sakhyaṃ kapisakhyaṃ tu śoṣiṇām
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         unmāde rākṣasaiḥ pretair apasmāre pravartanam ||68|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.69"><l> mehātisāriṇāṃ toyapānaṃ snehasya kuṣṭhinām |
                        <caesura/>
                         gulmeṣu sthāvarotpattiḥ koṣṭhe, mūrdhni śiroruji ||69|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.70"><l> śaṣkulībhakṣaṇaṃ chardyāmadhvā śvāsapipāsayoḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         hāridraṃ bhojanaṃ vā'pi yasya syāt pāṇḍurogiṇaḥ ||70|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.71ab"><l> raktapittī pibed yas tu śoṇitaṃ sa vinaśyati </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.71cd"><l> svapnān evaṃvidhān dṛṣṭvā prātar utthāya
                        yatnavān ||71|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.72"><l> dadyān māṣāṃs tilāṃ llohaṃ viprebhyaḥ kāñcanaṃ
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         japec cāpi śubhān mantrān gāyatrīṃ tripadāṃ tathā ||72|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.73"><l> dṛṣṭvā tu prathame yāme svapyād dhyātvā punaḥ
                        śubham |
                        <caesura/>
                         japed vā'nyatamaṃ vedaṃ brahmacārī samāhitaḥ ||73|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.74"><l> na cācakṣīta kasmaicid dṛṣṭvā svapnam
                        aśobhanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         devatāyatane caiva vased rātritrayaṃ tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         viprāṃś ca pūjayen nityaṃ duḥsvapnāt pravimucyate ||74|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.75"><l> ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi praśastaṃ
                        svapnadarśanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         devān dvijān govṛṣabhān jīvataḥ suhṛdo nṛpān ||75|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.76"><l> samiddham agniṃ sādhūṃś ca nirmalāni jalāni ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         paśyet kalyāṇalābhāya vyādher apagamāya ca ||76|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.77"><l> māṃsaṃ matsyān srajaḥ śvetā vāsāṃsi ca phalāni
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         labheta dhanalābhāya vyādher apagamāya ca ||77||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.78"><l> mahāprāsādasaphalavṛkṣavāraṇaparvatān |
                        <caesura/>
                         ārohed dravyalābhāya vyādher apagamāya ca ||78||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.79"><l> nadīnadasamudrāṃś ca kṣubhitān kaluṣodakān |
                        <caesura/>
                         taret kalyāṇalābhāya vyādher apagamāya ca ||79|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.80"><l> urago vā jalauko vā bhramaro vā'pi yaṃ daśet |
                        <caesura/>
                         ārogyaṃ nirdiśet tasya dhanalābhaṃ ca buddhimān ||80||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.81"><l> evaṃ rūpāñ śubhān svapnān yaḥ paśyedvyādhito
                        naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa dīrghāyur iti jñeyas tasmai karma samācaret ||81||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        viparītāviparītasvapnanidarśanīyo nāmaikonatriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ
                        ||29|| </l></lg>
                </div>


                <div n="30" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.1"><l> athātaḥ pañcendriyārthavipratipattim adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="221" n="137a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.3"><l> śarīraśīlayor yasya prakṛter vikṛtir bhavet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tat tv ariṣṭaṃ samāsena, vyāsatas tu nibodha me ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.4"><l> śṛṇoti vividhāñ śabdān yo divyānām abhāvataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         samudrapurameghānām asampattau ca niḥsvanān ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.5"><l> tān svanānnāvagṛhṇāti manyate cānyaśabdavat |
                        <caesura/>
                         grāmyāraṇyasvanāṃś cāpi viparītāñ śṛṇoti ca ||5||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.6"><l> dviṣacchabdeṣu ramate suhṛcchabdeṣu kupyati |
                        <caesura/>
                         na śṛṇoti ca yo 'kasmāttaṃ bruvanti gatāyuṣam ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.7"><l> yas tūṣṇam iva gṛhṇāti śītamuṣṇaṃ ca śītavat |
                        <caesura/>
                         sañjātaśītapiḍako yaś ca dāhena pīḍyate ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.8"><l> uṣṇagātro 'timātraṃ ca yaḥ śītena pravepate |
                        <caesura/>
                         prahārān nābhijānāti yo 'ṅgacchedamathāpi vā ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.9"><l> pāṃśunevāvakīrṇāni yaś ca gātrāṇi manyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         varṇānyatā vā rājyo vā yasya gātre bhavanti hi ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.10"><l> snātānuliptaṃ yaṃ cāpi bhajante nīlamakṣikāḥ
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="222" n="138a"/> sugandhirvā'ti yo 'kasmāttaṃ bruvanti
                        gatāyuṣam ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.11"><l> viparītena gṛhṇāti rasān yaś copayojitān |
                        <caesura/>
                         upayuktāḥ kramādyasya rasā doṣābhivṛddhaye ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.12"><l> yasya doṣāgnisāmyaṃ ca kuryur mithyopayojitāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         yo vā rasān na saṃvetti gatāsuṃ taṃ pracakṣate ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.13"><l> sugandhaṃ vetti durgandhaṃ durgandhasya
                        sugandhitām |
                        <caesura/>
                         gṛhṇīte vā'nyathā gandhaṃ śānte dīpe ca nīrujaḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.14"><l> yo vā gandhaṃ na jānāti gatāsuṃ taṃ vinirdiśet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dvandvānyuṣṇahimādīni kālāvasthā diśas tathā ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.15ab"><l> viparītena gṛhṇāti bhāvān anyāṃś ca yo naraḥ </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.15cd"><l> divā jyotīṃṣi yaś cāpi jvalitānīva paśyati
                        ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.16"><l> rātrau sūryaṃ jvalantaṃ vā divā vā
                        candravarcasam |
                        <caesura/>
                         ameghopaplave yaś ca śakracāpataḍidguṇān ||16||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.17"><l> taḍittvato 'sitān yo vā nirmale gagane ghanān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vimānayānaprāsādair yaś ca saṅkulamambaram ||17||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.18ab"><l> yaś cānilaṃ mūrtimantam antarikṣaṃ ca
                        paśyati | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.18cd"><l> dhūmanīhāravāsobhir āvṛtām iva medinīm
                        ||18||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.19"><l> pradīptam iva lokaṃ ca yo vā plutam ivāmbhasā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhūmim aṣṭāpadākārāṃ lekhābhir yaś ca paśyati ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.20"><l> na paśyati sanakṣatrāṃ yaś ca devīm arundhatīm
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhruvam ākāśagaṅgāṃ vā taṃ vadanti gatāyuṣam ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.21"><l> jyotsnādarśoṣṇatoyeṣu chāyāṃ yaś ca na paśyati
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         paśyaty ekāṅgahīnāṃ vā vikṛtāṃ vā'nyasattvajām ||21||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.22"><l> śvakākakaṅkagṛdhrāṇāṃ pretānāṃ yakṣarakṣasām |
                        <caesura/>
                         piśācoraganāgānāṃ bhūtānāṃ vikṛtām api ||22||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.23"><l> yo vā mayūrakaṇṭhābhaṃ vidhūmaṃ vahnim īkṣate
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         āturasya bhaven mṛtyuḥ svastho vyādhim avāpnuyāt ||23||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        pañcendriyārthavipratipattirnāma triṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||30|| </l></lg>
                </div>


                <div n="31" type="adhyāya">

                    <pb facs="223" n="139a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.1"><l> athātaś chāyāvipratipattim adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.3"><l> śyāvā lohitikā nīlā pītikā vā'pi mānavam |
                        <caesura/>
                         abhidravanti yaṃ chāyāḥ sa parāsur asaṃśayam ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.4"><l> hrīr apakramate yasya prabhāsmṛtidhṛtiśriyaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         akasmād yaṃ bhajante vā sa gatāsur asaṃśayam ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.5"><l> yasyādharauṣṭhaḥ patitaḥ kṣiptaś cordhvaṃ
                        tathottaraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ubhau vā jāmbavābhāsau durlabhaṃ tasya jīvitam ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.6"><l> āraktā daśanā yasya śyāvā vā syuḥ patanti vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         khañjanapratimā vā'pi taṃ gatāyuṣam ādiśet ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.7"><l> kṛṣṇā stabdhā'valiptā vā jihvā śūnā ca yasya
                        vai |
                        <caesura/>
                         karkaśā vā bhaved yasya so 'cirād vijahāty asūn ||7||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.8"><l> kuṭilā sphuṭitā vā'pi śuṣkā vā yasya nāsikā |
                        <caesura/>
                         avasphūrjati magnā vā na sa jīvati mānavaḥ ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.9"><l> saṅkṣipte viṣame stabdhe rakte sraste ca locane
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         syātāṃ vā prasrute yasya sa gatāyurnaro dhruvam ||9||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.10"><l> keśāḥ sīmantino yasya saṅkṣipte vinate bhruvau
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         luṇḍanti cākṣipakṣmāṇi so 'cirādyāti mṛtyave ||10||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="224" n="140a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.11"><l> nāharaty annam āsyasthaṃ na dhārayati yaḥ
                        śiraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ekāgradṛṣṭir mūḍhātmā sadyaḥ prāṇān jahāti saḥ ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.12"><l> balavān durbalo vā'pi sammohaṃ yo 'dhigacchati
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         utthāpyamāno bahuśastaṃ pakvaṃ bhiṣagādiśet ||12||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.13"><l> uttānaḥ sarvadā śete pādau vikurute ca yaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viprasāraṇaśīlo vā na sa jīvati mānavaḥ ||13||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.14"><l> śītapādakarocchvāsaś chinnocchvāsaś ca yo
                        bhavet |
                        <caesura/>
                         kākocchvāsaś ca yo martyas taṃ dhīraḥ parivarjayet ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.15"><l> nidrā na chidyate yasya yo vā jāgarti sarvadā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         muhyed vā vaktukāmaś ca pratyākhyeyaḥ sa jānatā ||15||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.16"><l> uttarauṣṭhaṃ ca yo lihyād utkārāṃś ca karoti
                        yaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pretair vā bhāṣate sārdhaṃ pretarūpaṃ tam ādiśet ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.17"><l> khebhyaḥ saromakūpebhyo yasya raktaṃ
                        pravartate |
                        <caesura/>
                         puruṣasyāviṣārtasya sadyo jahyāt sa jīvitam ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.18"><l> vātāṣṭhīlā tu hṛdaye yasyor dhvam anuyāyinī |
                        <caesura/>
                         rujānnavidveṣakarī sa parāsur asaṃśayam ||18||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.19"><l> ananyopadravakṛtaḥ śophaḥ pādasamutthitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         puruṣaṃ hanti, nārīṃ tu mukhajo guhyajo dvayam ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.20"><l> atisāro jvaro hikkā chardiḥ śūnāṇḍameḍhratā |
                        <caesura/>
                         śvāsinaḥ kāsino vā'pi yasya taṃ kṣīṇam ādiśet ||20||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.21"><l> svedo dāhaś ca balavān hikkā śvāsaś ca mānavam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         balavantam api prāṇair viyuñjanti na saṃśayaḥ ||21||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.22"><l> śyāvā jihvā bhaved yasya savyaṃ cākṣi
                        nimajjati |
                        <caesura/>
                         mukhaṃ ca jāyate pūti yasya taṃ parivarjayet ||22||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.23"><l> vaktramāpūryate 'śrūṇāṃ svidyataś caraṇāv
                        ubhau |
                        <caesura/>
                         cakṣuś cākulatāṃ yāti yamarāṣṭraṃ gamiṣyataḥ ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.24"><l> atimātraṃ laghūni syur gātrāṇi gurukāṇi vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         yasyākasmāt sa vijñeyo gantā vaivasvatālayam ||24||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.25"><l> paṅkamatsyavasātailaghṛtagandhāṃś ca ye narāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         mṛṣṭagandhāṃś ca ye vānti gantāraste yamālayam ||25||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.26"><l> yūkā lalāṭam āyānti baliṃ nāśnanti vāyasāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yeṣāṃ cāpi ratir nāsti yātāraste yamālayam ||26||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.27"><l> jvarātisāraśophāḥ syur yasyānyonyāvasādinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakṣīṇabalamāṃsasya nāsau śakyaścikitsitam ||27||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.28"><l> kṣīṇasya yasya kṣuttṛṣṇe hṛdyair miṣṭair
                        hitais tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         na śāmyato 'nnapānaiś ca tasya mṛtyur upasthitaḥ ||28||</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.29"><l> pravāhikā śiraḥśūlaṃ koṣṭhaśūlaṃ ca dāruṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         pipāsā balahāniś ca tasya mṛtyur upasthitaḥ ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="225" n="141a"/>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.30"><l> viṣameṇopacāreṇa karmabhiś ca purākṛtaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         anity atvāc ca jantūnāṃ jīvitaṃ nidhanaṃ vrajet ||30|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.31"><l> pretā bhūtāḥ piśācāś ca rakṣāṃsi vividhāni ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         maraṇābhimukhaṃ nityam upasarpanti mānavam ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.32"><l> tāni bheṣajavīryāṇi pratighnanti jighāṃsayā |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmān moghāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā bhavanty eva gatāyuṣām || 32 ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        chāyāvipratipattirnāmaikatriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||31|| </l></lg>
                </div>

            </div>

        </body>
